Sim — abaixo está o código completo, mantendo o que você já tinha e acrescentando só o que faltava para o botão do hero funcionar corretamente quando:

o cliente apaga todo o texto

o botão não some

ao digitar novamente, o texto continua com o estilo correto

clicar no botão continua permitindo editar o texto

clicar na imagem continua abrindo a escolha de imagem

o que já estava funcionando permanece

O ajuste principal foi:

remover document.body.contentEditable = 'true'

deixar contenteditable apenas onde precisa

no botão, usar:

<a class="cta-button">

<span class="cta-button-text" contenteditable="true">...</span>

adicionar CSS e JS para o botão não perder aparência quando o texto for apagado

Código completo
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useResult } from "./ResultContext";
import { useLocation, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

const meusProdutosDoBanco = [
    { id: 1, url: "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1523275335684-37898b6baf30?q=80" },
    { id: 2, url: "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1505740420928-5e560c06d30e?q=80" },
    { id: 3, url: "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1441986300917-64674bd600d8?q=80" },
];

const COMPONENT_LIBRARY = {
    hero: `
    <section class="luxury-hero added-section" style="padding: 100px 20px; color: white; min-height: 500px; display: flex; align-items: center; background: #1a1a1a; background-size: cover; background-position: center; background-repeat: no-repeat;">
        <div style="max-width: 1100px; margin: 0 auto; width: 100%;">
            <h1 contenteditable="true" style="font-size: 48px; margin-bottom: 20px; font-family: serif;">Título do Seu Site</h1>
            <p contenteditable="true" style="font-size: 20px; opacity: 0.9; max-width: 600px; margin-bottom: 30px;">Descreva seu valor principal aqui de forma clara e elegante.</p>

            <a
                class="cta-button"
                href="javascript:void(0)"
                style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; padding: 15px 40px; background: #b8917b; color: white; border-radius: 4px; font-weight: bold; text-decoration: none; font-size: 16px; border: none; cursor: pointer;"
            >
                <span class="cta-button-text" contenteditable="true">CLIQUE AQUI</span>
            </a>
        </div>
    </section>`,
    vitrine: `
        <section class="added-section" style="padding: 60px 20px; background: #fff;">
            <div style="max-width: 1200px; margin: 0 auto; text-align: center;">
                <h2 contenteditable="true" style="font-family: serif; font-size: 32px;">Nossos Destaques</h2>
                <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(250px, 1fr)); gap: 25px; margin-top: 30px;">
                    <div class="product-card" style="background: #fff; border: 1px solid #eee; border-radius: 12px; overflow: hidden; display: flex; flex-direction: column;">
                        <div class="product-img" style="height: 250px; width: 100%; background-image: url('https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1523275335684-37898b6baf30?q=80'); background-size: cover; background-position: center; cursor:pointer;"></div>
                        <div style="padding: 15px;">
                            <h4 contenteditable="true" style="margin: 0 0 10px; font-size: 18px;">Nome do Produto</h4>
                            <p style="color: #b8917b; font-weight: bold; font-size: 20px; margin-bottom: 15px;">R$ 00,00</p>
                            <button class="buy-button" style="width: 100%; padding: 12px; background: #b8917b; color: white; border: none; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: bold;">🛒 Comprar Agora</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>`,
    textoImagem: `
        <section class="added-section" style="padding: 80px 20px; background: #f9f9f9;">
            <div style="max-width: 1100px; margin: 0 auto; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 50px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                <div style="flex: 1; min-width: 300px;">
                    <h2 contenteditable="true" style="font-size: 36px; font-family: serif;">Sua História</h2>
                    <p contenteditable="true" style="color: #666; line-height: 1.6; font-size: 18px;">Descreva seus serviços ou missão aqui de forma detalhada.</p>
                </div>
                <div style="flex: 1; min-width: 300px; height: 400px; border-radius: 15px; overflow: hidden;">
                    <img src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1441986300917-64674bd600d8?q=80" style="width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; cursor:pointer;" />
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>`,
    depoimentos: `
        <section class="added-section" style="padding: 60px 20px; background: #fff;">
            <div style="max-width: 1000px; margin: 0 auto; text-align: center;">
                <h2 contenteditable="true" style="font-family: serif; font-size: 28px; margin-bottom: 40px;">O que dizem os clientes</h2>
                <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(280px, 1fr)); gap: 30px;">
                    <div style="padding: 30px; background: #fdf8f5; border-radius: 20px; border: 1px solid #f1e4dc;">
                        <p contenteditable="true" style="font-style: italic; color: #555;">"Excelente atendimento e qualidade impecável."</p>
                        <h4 contenteditable="true" style="margin-top: 20px; color: #b8917b;">— Cliente Satisfeito</h4>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>`,
    faq: `
        <section class="added-section" style="padding: 60px 20px; background: #f9f9f9;">
            <div style="max-width: 800px; margin: 0 auto;">
                <h2 contenteditable="true" style="text-align: center; font-family: serif; margin-bottom: 40px;">Dúvidas Frequentes</h2>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 20px; border-bottom: 1px solid #ddd; padding-bottom: 10px;">
                    <h4 contenteditable="true">Como funciona a entrega?</h4>
                    <p contenteditable="true" style="color: #777; font-size: 14px;">Entregamos em todo o Brasil com segurança.</p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>`,
    rodape: `
        <footer class="added-section" style="padding: 60px 20px; background: #1a1a1a; color: #fff; border-top: 1px solid #333;">
            <div style="max-width: 1100px; margin: 0 auto; display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(250px, 1fr)); gap: 40px;">
                <div>
                    <div class="logo-placeholder" style="width: 150px; height: 60px; background: #333; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; border: 1px dashed #666; cursor: pointer; margin-bottom: 20px;">
                        <span style="font-size: 12px; color: #999;">Clique p/ Logo</span>
                    </div>
                    <p contenteditable="true" style="font-size: 14px; color: #aaa;">Sua marca, sua história.</p>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <h4 style="color: #b8917b; margin-bottom: 20px;">Redes Sociais</h4>
                    <div class="social-links" style="display: flex; gap: 10px;">
                        <a href="#" data-name="whatsapp" class="social-icon" style="color:#fff; text-decoration:none; font-size:12px; border:1px solid #444; padding:5px 10px; border-radius:4px; opacity: 0.3;">WhatsApp</a>
                        <a href="#" data-name="instagram" class="social-icon" style="color:#fff; text-decoration:none; font-size:12px; border:1px solid #444; padding:5px 10px; border-radius:4px; opacity: 0.3;">Instagram</a>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </footer>`,
};

export default function Preview() {
    const { result: contextResult } = useResult();
    const location = useLocation();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const iframeRef = useRef<HTMLIFrameElement>(null);

    const result = location.state || contextResult || { html: "", css: "" };
    console.log("resultadoooooooooooooooo ", result);

    const [isGalleryOpen, setIsGalleryOpen] = useState(false);
    const [activeElementId, setActiveElementId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [heroBg1, setHeroBg1] = useState("#1a1a1a");
    const [heroBg2, setHeroBg2] = useState("#3a2b22");
    const [heroDirection, setHeroDirection] = useState("to right");

    const applyStyle = (command: string, value: string | null = null) => {
        iframeRef.current?.contentDocument?.execCommand(command, false, value || "");
    };

    const addComponent = (key: keyof typeof COMPONENT_LIBRARY) => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        if (doc) {
            const container = doc.querySelector(".luxury-container") || doc.body;
            const tempDiv = doc.createElement("div");
            tempDiv.innerHTML = COMPONENT_LIBRARY[key];
            container.appendChild(tempDiv.firstElementChild!);
        }
    };

    const updateHeroBackground = (style: string) => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        const hero = doc?.querySelector(".luxury-hero") as HTMLElement | null;
        if (hero) {
            hero.style.backgroundImage = style;
            hero.style.backgroundColor = "transparent";
            hero.style.backgroundSize = "cover";
            hero.style.backgroundPosition = "center";
            hero.style.backgroundRepeat = "no-repeat";
        }
    };

    const handleHeroImageUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (file) {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = (event) => {
                const res = event.target?.result;
                if (typeof res === "string") updateHeroBackground(`url(${res})`);
            };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        }
    };

    const changeHeroAlignment = (align: "left" | "center" | "right") => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        const hero = doc?.querySelector(".luxury-hero") as HTMLElement | null;
        if (hero) {
            hero.classList.remove("hero-align-left", "hero-align-center", "hero-align-right");
            hero.classList.add(`hero-align-${align}`);
        }
    };

    const selectPhotoFromDatabase = (url: string) => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        if (doc && activeElementId) {
            const el = doc.getElementById(activeElementId) as HTMLElement | null;
            if (el) {
                if (el.tagName === "IMG") {
                    (el as HTMLImageElement).src = url;
                } else {
                    el.style.backgroundImage = `url(${url})`;
                    el.style.backgroundSize = "cover";
                    el.style.backgroundPosition = "center";
                }
                setIsGalleryOpen(false);
            }
        }
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        updateHeroBackground(`linear-gradient(${heroDirection}, ${heroBg1}, ${heroBg2})`);
    }, [heroBg1, heroBg2, heroDirection]);

    useEffect(() => {
        const handleMessage = (event: MessageEvent) => {
            if (event.data.type === "OPEN_GALLERY") {
                setActiveElementId(event.data.elementId);
                setIsGalleryOpen(true);
            }
        };
        window.addEventListener("message", handleMessage);
        return () => window.removeEventListener("message", handleMessage);
    }, []);

    const handleSave = () => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        if (doc) {
            const cleanHtml = doc.body.cloneNode(true) as HTMLElement;

            cleanHtml.querySelectorAll(".del-btn").forEach((btn) => btn.remove());

            cleanHtml.querySelectorAll("[contenteditable]").forEach((el) => {
                el.removeAttribute("contenteditable");
                (el as HTMLElement).style.outline = "none";
            });

            const content =
                cleanHtml.querySelector(".luxury-container")?.innerHTML || cleanHtml.innerHTML;

            const dataToSave = {
                html: content,
                css: result.css,
                usuarioId: "user_2026_99",
                condominioId: null,
            };

            localStorage.setItem("landing_page_draft", JSON.stringify(dataToSave));
            alert("Landing Page salva com sucesso!");
        }
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        const iframe = iframeRef.current;
        if (!iframe || !result.html) return;

        let bodyContent = result.html;
        const bodyMatch = bodyContent.match(/<body[^>]*>([\s\S]*)<\/body>/i);
        if (bodyMatch && bodyMatch[1]) {
            bodyContent = bodyMatch[1];
        }

        const fullHtml = `
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
    <head>
        <meta charset="UTF-8">
        <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Playfair+Display:wght@600&family=Great+Vibes&family=Inter:wght@400;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
        <style>
            ${result.css || ""}
            body {
                margin: 0;
                padding: 0;
                min-height: 100vh;
                font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            }

            .luxury-container {
                min-height: 100vh;
                width: 100%;
                position: relative;
            }

            img {
                max-width: 100%;
                height: auto;
            }

            [contenteditable]:focus {
                outline: 2px solid #b8917b;
                background: rgba(184, 145, 123, 0.05);
            }

            .del-btn {
                opacity: 0;
                transition: 0.2s;
                position: absolute;
                top: 10px;
                right: 10px;
                background: red;
                color: white;
                border: none;
                width: 25px;
                height: 25px;
                cursor: pointer;
                border-radius: 50%;
                z-index: 100;
            }

            section:hover .del-btn,
            footer:hover .del-btn {
                opacity: 1;
            }

            section,
            footer {
                position: relative;
            }

            .hero-align-left > div {
                text-align: left;
            }

            .hero-align-center > div {
                text-align: center;
            }

            .hero-align-right > div {
                text-align: right;
            }

            .cta-button {
                display: inline-flex !important;
                align-items: center;
                justify-content: center;
                min-width: 140px;
                min-height: 50px;
                padding: 15px 40px;
                background: #b8917b;
                color: #fff !important;
                border-radius: 4px;
                font-weight: bold;
                font-size: 16px;
                text-decoration: none !important;
                border: none;
                cursor: pointer;
                vertical-align: middle;
            }

            .cta-button-text {
                display: inline-block;
                min-width: 24px;
                min-height: 20px;
                color: inherit !important;
                font: inherit;
                font-weight: inherit;
                line-height: inherit;
                white-space: nowrap;
                outline: none;
            }

            .cta-button-text:empty::before {
                content: "CLIQUE AQUI";
                opacity: 0.55;
                color: inherit;
                font: inherit;
                font-weight: inherit;
            }

            .product-img:hover,
            img:hover,
            .logo-placeholder:hover,
            .cta-button:hover {
                outline: 2px dashed rgba(184, 145, 123, 0.65);
                outline-offset: 2px;
            }
        </style>
    </head>
    <body>
        <main class="luxury-container">
            ${bodyContent}
        </main>
        <script>
            document.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
                const target = e.target;

                if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("cta-button-text")) {
                    if (e.key === "Enter") {
                        e.preventDefault();
                    }
                }
            });

            document.addEventListener("paste", function (e) {
                const target = e.target;

                if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("cta-button-text")) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const text = (e.clipboardData || window.clipboardData).getData("text");
                    document.execCommand("insertText", false, text);
                }
            });

            document.addEventListener("blur", function (e) {
                const target = e.target;
                if (
                    target.classList &&
                    target.classList.contains("cta-button-text") &&
                    target.textContent.trim() === ""
                ) {
                    target.textContent = "";
                }
            }, true);

            document.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
                const target = e.target;

                if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("product-img")) {
                    const el = target;
                    const id = el.id || "img-" + Date.now();
                    el.id = id;
                    window.parent.postMessage({ type: "OPEN_GALLERY", elementId: id }, "*");
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".product-img")) {
                    const el = target.closest(".product-img");
                    const id = el.id || "img-" + Date.now();
                    el.id = id;
                    window.parent.postMessage({ type: "OPEN_GALLERY", elementId: id }, "*");
                    return;
                }

                if (target.tagName === "IMG") {
                    const input = document.createElement("input");
                    input.type = "file";
                    input.accept = "image/*";
                    input.onchange = function (ev) {
                        const file = ev.target.files && ev.target.files[0];
                        if (!file) return;

                        const reader = new FileReader();
                        reader.onload = function (re) {
                            target.src = re.target.result;
                        };
                        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                    };
                    input.click();
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".logo-placeholder")) {
                    const placeholder = target.closest(".logo-placeholder");
                    const input = document.createElement("input");
                    input.type = "file";
                    input.accept = "image/*";
                    input.onchange = function (ev) {
                        const file = ev.target.files && ev.target.files[0];
                        if (!file) return;

                        const reader = new FileReader();
                        reader.onload = function (re) {
                            placeholder.innerHTML = '<img src="' + re.target.result + '" style="max-width:100%; max-height:100%; width:auto; height:auto; object-fit:contain;" />';
                        };
                        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                    };
                    input.click();
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".cta-button")) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const button = target.classList && target.classList.contains("cta-button-text")
                        ? target
                        : target.closest(".cta-button").querySelector(".cta-button-text");

                    if (button) {
                        button.focus();

                        const range = document.createRange();
                        range.selectNodeContents(button);
                        range.collapse(false);

                        const sel = window.getSelection();
                        sel.removeAllRanges();
                        sel.addRange(range);
                    }
                    return;
                }
            }, true);

            document.addEventListener("mouseover", function (e) {
                const section = e.target.closest && e.target.closest("section, footer");
                if (section && !section.querySelector(".del-btn")) {
                    const btn = document.createElement("button");
                    btn.innerHTML = "✕";
                    btn.className = "del-btn";
                    btn.contentEditable = "false";
                    btn.onclick = function (ev) {
                        ev.preventDefault();
                        ev.stopPropagation();
                        section.remove();
                    };
                    section.appendChild(btn);
                }
            });
        </script>
    </body>
</html>`;

        iframe.srcdoc = fullHtml;
    }, [result.html, result.css]);

    return (
        <div style={containerStyle}>
            <div style={sidebarStyle}>
                <h3 style={{ fontSize: "16px", marginBottom: "10px" }}>Estúdio Design</h3>

                <div style={groupStyle}>
                    <label style={labelStyle}>Adicionar Seções</label>
                    <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: "8px" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("hero")} style={blockBtnStyle}>➕ Hero</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("vitrine")} style={blockBtnStyle}>➕ Vitrine</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("textoImagem")} style={blockBtnStyle}>➕ Texto+Foto</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("depoimentos")} style={blockBtnStyle}>💬 Depoimentos</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("faq")} style={blockBtnStyle}>❓ FAQ</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("rodape")} style={blockBtnStyle}>👣 Rodapé</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={groupStyle}>
                    <label style={labelStyle}>Fundo do Topo</label>
                    <input
                        type="file"
                        accept="image/*"
                        onChange={handleHeroImageUpload}
                        style={{ fontSize: "11px", marginBottom: "5px" }}
                    />
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "10px", alignItems: "center" }}>
                        <input type="color" value={heroBg1} onChange={(e) => setHeroBg1(e.target.value)} style={colorCircle} />
                        <input type="color" value={heroBg2} onChange={(e) => setHeroBg2(e.target.value)} style={colorCircle} />
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "5px", marginTop: "5px" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => setHeroDirection("to right")} style={heroDirection === "to right" ? activeSmallBtn : smallBtnStyle}>👉</button>
                        <button onClick={() => setHeroDirection("to left")} style={heroDirection === "to left" ? activeSmallBtn : smallBtnStyle}>👈</button>
                        <button onClick={() => setHeroDirection("to bottom")} style={heroDirection === "to bottom" ? activeSmallBtn : smallBtnStyle}>👇</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={groupStyle}>
                    <label style={labelStyle}>Alinhamento</label>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "5px" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => changeHeroAlignment("left")} style={smallBtnStyle}>⬅️</button>
                        <button onClick={() => changeHeroAlignment("center")} style={smallBtnStyle}>⏺️</button>
                        <button onClick={() => changeHeroAlignment("right")} style={smallBtnStyle}>➡️</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={{ marginTop: "auto" }}>
                    <button onClick={() => navigate("/publicado")} style={btnStyle}>👁️ Ver Resultado</button>
                    <button
                        onClick={handleSave}
                        style={{ ...btnStyle, background: "#10b981", color: "white", border: "none" }}
                    >
                        💾 Salvar Agora
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div style={mainAreaStyle}>
                <div style={toolbarStyle}>
                    <button onClick={() => applyStyle("bold")} style={toolbarBtn}><b>B</b></button>
                    <button onClick={() => applyStyle("italic")} style={toolbarBtn}><i>I</i></button>
                    <input type="color" onChange={(e) => applyStyle("foreColor", e.target.value)} style={colorCircle} />
                </div>
                <iframe ref={iframeRef} style={iframeStyle} title="Editor" />
            </div>

            {isGalleryOpen && (
                <div style={modalOverlayStyle}>
                    <div style={modalContentStyle}>
                        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: "15px" }}>
                            <h3 style={{ margin: 0 }}>Galeria do Banco</h3>
                            <button
                                onClick={() => setIsGalleryOpen(false)}
                                style={{ border: "none", background: "none", cursor: "pointer", fontSize: "18px" }}
                            >
                                ✕
                            </button>
                        </div>
                        <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr 1fr", gap: "10px" }}>
                            {meusProdutosDoBanco.map((prod) => (
                                <img
                                    key={prod.id}
                                    src={prod.url}
                                    onClick={() => selectPhotoFromDatabase(prod.url)}
                                    style={galleryImgStyle}
                                    alt={`Produto ${prod.id}`}
                                />
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

// Estilos
const containerStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    display: "flex",
    background: "#f1f5f9",
    height: "100vh",
    fontFamily: "sans-serif",
};

const sidebarStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    width: "280px",
    background: "white",
    padding: "20px",
    display: "flex",
    flexDirection: "column",
    gap: "15px",
    borderRight: "1px solid #ddd",
};

const mainAreaStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    flex: 1,
    padding: "20px",
    display: "flex",
    flexDirection: "column",
};

const groupStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    display: "flex",
    flexDirection: "column",
    gap: "8px",
    padding: "12px",
    background: "#f8fafc",
    borderRadius: "8px",
};

const labelStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    fontSize: "11px",
    fontWeight: "bold",
    color: "#64748b",
    textTransform: "uppercase",
};

const colorCircle: React.CSSProperties = {
    width: "26px",
    height: "26px",
    border: "1px solid #ddd",
    borderRadius: "50%",
    cursor: "pointer",
    padding: "0",
};

const btnStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    width: "100%",
    padding: "12px",
    borderRadius: "8px",
    border: "1px solid #ddd",
    cursor: "pointer",
    fontWeight: "bold",
    marginBottom: "8px",
    background: "#f1f5f9",
};

const blockBtnStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    padding: "8px",
    background: "white",
    border: "1px solid #cbd5e1",
    borderRadius: "6px",
    cursor: "pointer",
    fontSize: "11px",
    textAlign: "left",
};

const smallBtnStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    flex: 1,
    padding: "8px",
    border: "1px solid #ddd",
    background: "white",
    cursor: "pointer",
    borderRadius: "4px",

O que foi corrigido sem apagar o resto
O que foi mantido:

adicionar seções

trocar imagem do topo

gradiente do hero

alinhamento do hero

toolbar com bold / italic / cor

clique nas imagens

logo upload

modal da galeria

salvar draft

O que foi acrescentado:

estrutura correta do botão do hero

CSS para o botão não sumir quando apagar o texto

placeholder visual no botão vazio

texto novo herdar cor, peso e fonte

bloqueio de Enter dentro do texto do botão

paste limpo no botão

clique no botão focando o texto certo

remoção de document.body.contentEditable = 'true', que era a principal causa do problema

Observação importante
No seu useEffect, você tinha:

if (!iframe || !result.html) return;
Isso impede o editor de carregar quando result.html vier vazio.
Se você quiser que o preview abra mesmo sem HTML inicial, troque por:

if (!iframe) return;
e deixe:

const bodyContent = result.html || "";
Porque hoje, se result.html estiver vazio, o iframe nem monta.

Se você quiser, no próximo passo eu posso te devolver uma versão com edição da cor do botão do hero pela sidebar sem mexer no resto.


sem isso 
            document.body.contentEditable = 'true'; ele não deixa eu editar os textos 

Exato — esse é o ponto.

Sem:

document.body.contentEditable = 'true';
só os elementos que tiverem contenteditable="true" vão aceitar edição.

Então o problema não é que o body precisa ser editável. O problema é que parte do HTML que entra no iframe não está vindo com contenteditable nos textos.

Quando você liga o body.contentEditable, tudo vira editável, mas isso também quebra o botão, links, estrutura e inserção de texto.

O jeito certo
Você tem 2 cenários no seu editor:

1. Componentes criados por você
Esses já vêm com:

<h1 contenteditable="true">...</h1>
<p contenteditable="true">...</p>
<span class="cta-button-text" contenteditable="true">...</span>
Então funcionam sem precisar do body.contentEditable.

2. HTML vindo da IA / banco
Esse HTML pode vir sem contenteditable.
Aí realmente você não consegue editar.

Solução correta: marcar automaticamente os textos editáveis
Em vez de fazer o body inteiro editável, você pode, depois que o iframe carregar, adicionar contenteditable="true" nos elementos de texto que você quer liberar.

Faça isso dentro do script do iframe
Adicione esta função:

<script>
    function enableEditableText() {
        const editableSelectors = [
            "h1", "h2", "h3", "h4", "h5", "h6",
            "p", "span", "small", "strong", "em",
            "li", "blockquote", "figcaption",
            ".cta-button-text"
        ];

        document.querySelectorAll(editableSelectors.join(",")).forEach((el) => {
            if (
                !el.closest(".del-btn") &&
                !el.classList.contains("social-icon") &&
                !el.classList.contains("buy-button") &&
                !el.classList.contains("cta-button")
            ) {
                el.setAttribute("contenteditable", "true");
            }
        });
    }

    enableEditableText();
</script>
Por que isso resolve
Assim você consegue:

editar títulos, parágrafos e spans

manter o botão visual intacto

evitar que o navegador destrua a estrutura do HTML

continuar permitindo formatação com execCommand

O que você deve trocar no seu código
Remova isto:
document.body.contentEditable = 'true';
E coloque isto no lugar, dentro do <script>:
function enableEditableText() {
    const editableSelectors = [
        "h1", "h2", "h3", "h4", "h5", "h6",
        "p", "span", "small", "strong", "em",
        "li", "blockquote", "figcaption",
        ".cta-button-text"
    ];

    document.querySelectorAll(editableSelectors.join(",")).forEach((el) => {
        if (
            !el.closest(".del-btn") &&
            !el.classList.contains("social-icon") &&
            !el.classList.contains("buy-button") &&
            !el.classList.contains("cta-button")
        ) {
            el.setAttribute("contenteditable", "true");
        }
    });
}

enableEditableText();
Melhor ainda: reaplicar quando adicionar componente
Como você adiciona seções dinamicamente com addComponent, o ideal é também marcar os novos textos como editáveis logo após inserir.

Troque seu addComponent por este:

const addComponent = (key: keyof typeof COMPONENT_LIBRARY) => {
    const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
    if (doc) {
        const container = doc.querySelector(".luxury-container") || doc.body;
        const tempDiv = doc.createElement("div");
        tempDiv.innerHTML = COMPONENT_LIBRARY[key];

        const newEl = tempDiv.firstElementChild as HTMLElement | null;
        if (newEl) {
            container.appendChild(newEl);

            newEl.querySelectorAll("h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, p, span, small, strong, em, li, blockquote, figcaption, .cta-button-text")
                .forEach((el) => {
                    const htmlEl = el as HTMLElement;
                    if (
                        !htmlEl.classList.contains("social-icon") &&
                        !htmlEl.classList.contains("buy-button") &&
                        !htmlEl.classList.contains("cta-button")
                    ) {
                        htmlEl.setAttribute("contenteditable", "true");
                    }
                });
        }
    }
};
Trecho completo do script do iframe já ajustado
No seu fullHtml, pode usar assim:

<script>
    function enableEditableText() {
        const editableSelectors = [
            "h1", "h2", "h3", "h4", "h5", "h6",
            "p", "span", "small", "strong", "em",
            "li", "blockquote", "figcaption",
            ".cta-button-text"
        ];

        document.querySelectorAll(editableSelectors.join(",")).forEach((el) => {
            if (
                !el.closest(".del-btn") &&
                !el.classList.contains("social-icon") &&
                !el.classList.contains("buy-button") &&
                !el.classList.contains("cta-button")
            ) {
                el.setAttribute("contenteditable", "true");
            }
        });
    }

    enableEditableText();

    document.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
        const target = e.target;

        if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("cta-button-text")) {
            if (e.key === "Enter") {
                e.preventDefault();
            }
        }
    });

    document.addEventListener("paste", function (e) {
        const target = e.target;

        if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("cta-button-text")) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const text = (e.clipboardData || window.clipboardData).getData("text");
            document.execCommand("insertText", false, text);
        }
    });

    document.addEventListener("blur", function (e) {
        const target = e.target;
        if (
            target.classList &&
            target.classList.contains("cta-button-text") &&
            target.textContent.trim() === ""
        ) {
            target.textContent = "";
        }
    }, true);

    document.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
        const target = e.target;

        if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("product-img")) {
            const el = target;
            const id = el.id || "img-" + Date.now();
            el.id = id;
            window.parent.postMessage({ type: "OPEN_GALLERY", elementId: id }, "*");
            return;
        }

        if (target.closest && target.closest(".product-img")) {
            const el = target.closest(".product-img");
            const id = el.id || "img-" + Date.now();
            el.id = id;
            window.parent.postMessage({ type: "OPEN_GALLERY", elementId: id }, "*");
            return;
        }

        if (target.tagName === "IMG") {
            const input = document.createElement("input");
            input.type = "file";
            input.accept = "image/*";
            input.onchange = function (ev) {
                const file = ev.target.files && ev.target.files[0];
                if (!file) return;

                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = function (re) {
                    target.src = re.target.result;
                };
                reader.readAsDataURL(file);
            };
            input.click();
            return;
        }

        if (target.closest && target.closest(".logo-placeholder")) {
            const placeholder = target.closest(".logo-placeholder");
            const input = document.createElement("input");
            input.type = "file";
            input.accept = "image/*";
            input.onchange = function (ev) {
                const file = ev.target.files && ev.target.files[0];
                if (!file) return;

                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = function (re) {
                    placeholder.innerHTML = '<img src="' + re.target.result + '" style="max-width:100%; max-height:100%; width:auto; height:auto; object-fit:contain;" />';
                };
                reader.readAsDataURL(file);
            };
            input.click();
            return;
        }

        if (target.closest && target.closest(".cta-button")) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const buttonText = target.classList && target.classList.contains("cta-button-text")
                ? target
                : target.closest(".cta-button").querySelector(".cta-button-text");

            if (buttonText) {
                buttonText.focus();

                const range = document.createRange();
                range.selectNodeContents(buttonText);
                range.collapse(false);

                const sel = window.getSelection();
                sel.removeAllRanges();
                sel.addRange(range);
            }
            return;
        }
    }, true);

    document.addEventListener("mouseover", function (e) {
        const section = e.target.closest && e.target.closest("section, footer");
        if (section && !section.querySelector(".del-btn")) {
            const btn = document.createElement("button");
            btn.innerHTML = "✕";
            btn.className = "del-btn";
            btn.contentEditable = "false";
            btn.onclick = function (ev) {
                ev.preventDefault();
                ev.stopPropagation();
                section.remove();
            };
            section.appendChild(btn);
        }
    });
</script>
Resumo prático
Você estava usando o body.contentEditable como uma “muleta” para tornar tudo editável.
Mas o certo é:

deixar o body normal

ativar contenteditable só nos textos

aplicar isso automaticamente nos elementos vindos da IA

Assim você mantém a edição sem quebrar o botão.

Se você quiser, eu posso agora te devolver o arquivo completo já refeito com essa correção aplicada no seu código inteiro, pronto para colar.


nos cards também preciso alterar os valores os textos 

Perfeito — isso acontece porque nos cards da vitrine alguns textos não têm contenteditable="true".
Então o editor não permite modificar.

No seu código atual, o card está assim:

<h4 contenteditable="true">Nome do Produto</h4>
<p style="color: #b8917b; font-weight: bold;">R$ 00,00</p>
<button class="buy-button">🛒 Comprar Agora</button>
Somente o nome do produto é editável.

Correção correta
Precisamos tornar editável:

nome do produto

preço

texto do botão

Mas sem quebrar o botão.

Então fazemos igual ao hero:

botão mantém estilo

texto editável dentro de um <span>

Card corrigido

<div class="product-card" style="background: #fff; border: 1px solid #eee; border-radius: 12px; overflow: hidden; display: flex; flex-direction: column;">
    
    <div class="product-img"
        style="height: 250px; width: 100%; background-image: url('https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1523275335684-37898b6baf30?q=80'); background-size: cover; background-position: center; cursor:pointer;">
    </div>

    <div style="padding: 15px;">
        
        <h4 contenteditable="true"
            style="margin: 0 0 10px; font-size: 18px;">
            Nome do Produto
        </h4>

        <p contenteditable="true"
           class="product-price"
           style="color: #b8917b; font-weight: bold; font-size: 20px; margin-bottom: 15px;">
           R$ 00,00
        </p>

        <button class="buy-button"
            style="width: 100%; padding: 12px; background: #b8917b; color: white; border: none; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: bold;">

            <span class="buy-button-text" contenteditable="true">
                🛒 Comprar Agora
            </span>

        </button>

    </div>

</div>

}

    min-width: 10px;
    outline: none;
    color: inherit;
    font: inherit;
}

.buy-button-text:empty::before {
    content: "Comprar";
    opacity: 0.5;
}

    const target = e.target;

    if (
        target.classList &&
        (target.classList.contains("cta-button-text") ||
         target.classList.contains("buy-button-text"))
    ) {
        if (e.key === "Enter") {
            e.preventDefault();
        }
    }
});

export default function Preview() {
    const { result: contextResult } = useResult();
    const location = useLocation();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const iframeRef = useRef<HTMLIFrameElement>(null);

    const result = location.state || contextResult || { html: "", css: "" };
    console.log("resultadoooooooooooooooo ", result);

    const [isGalleryOpen, setIsGalleryOpen] = useState(false);
    const [activeElementId, setActiveElementId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [heroBg1, setHeroBg1] = useState("#1a1a1a");
    const [heroBg2, setHeroBg2] = useState("#3a2b22");
    const [heroDirection, setHeroDirection] = useState("to right");

    const applyStyle = (command: string, value: string | null = null) => {
        iframeRef.current?.contentDocument?.execCommand(command, false, value || "");
    };

    const addComponent = (key: keyof typeof COMPONENT_LIBRARY) => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        if (doc) {
            const container = doc.querySelector(".luxury-container") || doc.body;
            const tempDiv = doc.createElement("div");
            tempDiv.innerHTML = COMPONENT_LIBRARY[key];

            const newEl = tempDiv.firstElementChild as HTMLElement | null;
            if (newEl) {
                container.appendChild(newEl);

                newEl
                    .querySelectorAll(
                        "h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, p, span, small, strong, em, li, blockquote, figcaption, .cta-button-text, .buy-button-text, .product-price"
                    )
                    .forEach((el) => {
                        const htmlEl = el as HTMLElement;
                        if (
                            !htmlEl.classList.contains("social-icon") &&
                            !htmlEl.classList.contains("buy-button") &&
                            !htmlEl.classList.contains("cta-button")
                        ) {
                            htmlEl.setAttribute("contenteditable", "true");
                        }
                    });
            }
        }
    };

    const updateHeroBackground = (style: string) => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        const hero = doc?.querySelector(".luxury-hero") as HTMLElement | null;
        if (hero) {
            hero.style.backgroundImage = style;
            hero.style.backgroundColor = "transparent";
            hero.style.backgroundSize = "cover";
            hero.style.backgroundPosition = "center";
            hero.style.backgroundRepeat = "no-repeat";
        }
    };

    const handleHeroImageUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (file) {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = (event) => {
                const res = event.target?.result;
                if (typeof res === "string") updateHeroBackground(`url(${res})`);
            };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        }
    };

    const changeHeroAlignment = (align: "left" | "center" | "right") => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        const hero = doc?.querySelector(".luxury-hero") as HTMLElement | null;
        if (hero) {
            hero.classList.remove("hero-align-left", "hero-align-center", "hero-align-right");
            hero.classList.add(`hero-align-${align}`);
        }
    };

    const selectPhotoFromDatabase = (url: string) => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        if (doc && activeElementId) {
            const el = doc.getElementById(activeElementId) as HTMLElement | null;
            if (el) {
                if (el.tagName === "IMG") {
                    (el as HTMLImageElement).src = url;
                } else {
                    el.style.backgroundImage = `url(${url})`;
                    el.style.backgroundSize = "cover";
                    el.style.backgroundPosition = "center";
                }
                setIsGalleryOpen(false);
            }
        }
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        updateHeroBackground(`linear-gradient(${heroDirection}, ${heroBg1}, ${heroBg2})`);
    }, [heroBg1, heroBg2, heroDirection]);

    useEffect(() => {
        const handleMessage = (event: MessageEvent) => {
            if (event.data.type === "OPEN_GALLERY") {
                setActiveElementId(event.data.elementId);
                setIsGalleryOpen(true);
            }
        };
        window.addEventListener("message", handleMessage);
        return () => window.removeEventListener("message", handleMessage);
    }, []);

    const handleSave = () => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        if (doc) {
            const cleanHtml = doc.body.cloneNode(true) as HTMLElement;

            cleanHtml.querySelectorAll(".del-btn").forEach((btn) => btn.remove());

            cleanHtml.querySelectorAll("[contenteditable]").forEach((el) => {
                el.removeAttribute("contenteditable");
                (el as HTMLElement).style.outline = "none";
            });

            const content =
                cleanHtml.querySelector(".luxury-container")?.innerHTML || cleanHtml.innerHTML;

            const dataToSave = {
                html: content,
                css: result.css,
                usuarioId: "user_2026_99",
                condominioId: null,
            };

            localStorage.setItem("landing_page_draft", JSON.stringify(dataToSave));
            alert("Landing Page salva com sucesso!");
        }
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        const iframe = iframeRef.current;
        if (!iframe) return;

        let bodyContent = result.html || "";
        const bodyMatch = bodyContent.match(/<body[^>]*>([\s\S]*)<\/body>/i);
        if (bodyMatch && bodyMatch[1]) {
            bodyContent = bodyMatch[1];
        }

        const fullHtml = `
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
    <head>
        <meta charset="UTF-8">
        <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Playfair+Display:wght@600&family=Great+Vibes&family=Inter:wght@400;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
        <style>
            ${result.css || ""}
            body {
                margin: 0;
                padding: 0;
                min-height: 100vh;
                font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            }

            .luxury-container {
                min-height: 100vh;
                width: 100%;
                position: relative;
            }

            img {
                max-width: 100%;
                height: auto;
            }

            [contenteditable]:focus {
                outline: 2px solid #b8917b;
                background: rgba(184, 145, 123, 0.05);
            }

            .del-btn {
                opacity: 0;
                transition: 0.2s;
                position: absolute;
                top: 10px;
                right: 10px;
                background: red;
                color: white;
                border: none;
                width: 25px;
                height: 25px;
                cursor: pointer;
                border-radius: 50%;
                z-index: 100;
            }

            section:hover .del-btn,
            footer:hover .del-btn {
                opacity: 1;
            }

            section,
            footer {
                position: relative;
            }

            .hero-align-left > div {
                text-align: left;
            }

            .hero-align-center > div {
                text-align: center;
            }

            .hero-align-right > div {
                text-align: right;
            }

            .cta-button {
                display: inline-flex !important;
                align-items: center;
                justify-content: center;
                min-width: 140px;
                min-height: 50px;
                padding: 15px 40px;
                background: #b8917b;
                color: #fff !important;
                border-radius: 4px;
                font-weight: bold;
                font-size: 16px;
                text-decoration: none !important;
                border: none;
                cursor: pointer;
                vertical-align: middle;
            }

            .cta-button-text {
                display: inline-block;
                min-width: 24px;
                min-height: 20px;
                color: inherit !important;
                font: inherit;
                font-weight: inherit;
                line-height: inherit;
                white-space: nowrap;
                outline: none;
            }

            .cta-button-text:empty::before {
                content: "CLIQUE AQUI";
                opacity: 0.55;
                color: inherit;
                font: inherit;
                font-weight: inherit;
            }

            .buy-button {
                display: flex;
                justify-content: center;
                align-items: center;
                width: 100%;
                padding: 12px;
                background: #b8917b;
                color: white;
                border: none;
                cursor: pointer;
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-weight: bold;
            }

            .buy-button-text {
                display: inline-block;
                min-width: 10px;
                min-height: 18px;
                outline: none;
                color: inherit !important;
                font: inherit;
                font-weight: inherit;
                line-height: inherit;
                white-space: nowrap;
            }

            .buy-button-text:empty::before {
                content: "Comprar";
                opacity: 0.5;
                color: inherit;
                font: inherit;
                font-weight: inherit;
            }

            .product-img:hover,
            img:hover,
            .logo-placeholder:hover,
            .cta-button:hover,
            .buy-button:hover {
                outline: 2px dashed rgba(184, 145, 123, 0.65);
                outline-offset: 2px;
            }
        </style>
    </head>
    <body>
        <main class="luxury-container">
            ${bodyContent}
        </main>
        <script>
            function enableEditableText() {
                const editableSelectors = [
                    "h1", "h2", "h3", "h4", "h5", "h6",
                    "p", "span", "small", "strong", "em",
                    "li", "blockquote", "figcaption",
                    ".cta-button-text",
                    ".buy-button-text",
                    ".product-price"
                ];

                document.querySelectorAll(editableSelectors.join(",")).forEach((el) => {
                    if (
                        !el.closest(".del-btn") &&
                        !el.classList.contains("social-icon") &&
                        !el.classList.contains("buy-button") &&
                        !el.classList.contains("cta-button")
                    ) {
                        el.setAttribute("contenteditable", "true");
                    }
                });
            }

            enableEditableText();

            document.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
                const target = e.target;

                if (
                    target.classList &&
                    (
                        target.classList.contains("cta-button-text") ||
                        target.classList.contains("buy-button-text")
                    )
                ) {
                    if (e.key === "Enter") {
                        e.preventDefault();
                    }
                }
            });

            document.addEventListener("paste", function (e) {
                const target = e.target;

                if (
                    target.classList &&
                    (
                        target.classList.contains("cta-button-text") ||
                        target.classList.contains("buy-button-text")
                    )
                ) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const text = (e.clipboardData || window.clipboardData).getData("text");
                    document.execCommand("insertText", false, text);
                }
            });

            document.addEventListener("blur", function (e) {
                const target = e.target;

                if (
                    target.classList &&
                    target.classList.contains("cta-button-text") &&
                    target.textContent.trim() === ""
                ) {
                    target.textContent = "";
                }

                if (
                    target.classList &&
                    target.classList.contains("buy-button-text") &&
                    target.textContent.trim() === ""
                ) {
                    target.textContent = "";
                }
            }, true);

            document.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
                const target = e.target;

                if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("product-img")) {
                    const el = target;
                    const id = el.id || "img-" + Date.now();
                    el.id = id;
                    window.parent.postMessage({ type: "OPEN_GALLERY", elementId: id }, "*");
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".product-img")) {
                    const el = target.closest(".product-img");
                    const id = el.id || "img-" + Date.now();
                    el.id = id;
                    window.parent.postMessage({ type: "OPEN_GALLERY", elementId: id }, "*");
                    return;
                }

                if (target.tagName === "IMG") {
                    const input = document.createElement("input");
                    input.type = "file";
                    input.accept = "image/*";
                    input.onchange = function (ev) {
                        const file = ev.target.files && ev.target.files[0];
                        if (!file) return;

                        const reader = new FileReader();
                        reader.onload = function (re) {
                            target.src = re.target.result;
                        };
                        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                    };
                    input.click();
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".logo-placeholder")) {
                    const placeholder = target.closest(".logo-placeholder");
                    const input = document.createElement("input");
                    input.type = "file";
                    input.accept = "image/*";
                    input.onchange = function (ev) {
                        const file = ev.target.files && ev.target.files[0];
                        if (!file) return;

                        const reader = new FileReader();
                        reader.onload = function (re) {
                            placeholder.innerHTML = '<img src="' + re.target.result + '" style="max-width:100%; max-height:100%; width:auto; height:auto; object-fit:contain;" />';
                        };
                        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                    };
                    input.click();
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".cta-button")) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const buttonText = target.classList && target.classList.contains("cta-button-text")
                        ? target
                        : target.closest(".cta-button").querySelector(".cta-button-text");

                    if (buttonText) {
                        buttonText.focus();

                        const range = document.createRange();
                        range.selectNodeContents(buttonText);
                        range.collapse(false);

                        const sel = window.getSelection();
                        sel.removeAllRanges();
                        sel.addRange(range);
                    }
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".buy-button")) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const buttonText = target.classList && target.classList.contains("buy-button-text")
                        ? target
                        : target.closest(".buy-button").querySelector(".buy-button-text");

                    if (buttonText) {
                        buttonText.focus();

                        const range = document.createRange();
                        range.selectNodeContents(buttonText);
                        range.collapse(false);

                        const sel = window.getSelection();
                        sel.removeAllRanges();
                        sel.addRange(range);
                    }
                    return;
                }
            }, true);

            document.addEventListener("mouseover", function (e) {
                const section = e.target.closest && e.target.closest("section, footer");
                if (section && !section.querySelector(".del-btn")) {
                    const btn = document.createElement("button");
                    btn.innerHTML = "✕";
                    btn.className = "del-btn";
                    btn.contentEditable = "false";
                    btn.onclick = function (ev) {
                        ev.preventDefault();
                        ev.stopPropagation();
                        section.remove();
                    };
                    section.appendChild(btn);
                }
            });
        </script>
    </body>
</html>`;

        iframe.srcdoc = fullHtml;
    }, [result.html, result.css]);

    return (
        <div style={containerStyle}>
            <div style={sidebarStyle}>
                <h3 style={{ fontSize: "16px", marginBottom: "10px" }}>Estúdio Design</h3>

                <div style={groupStyle}>
                    <label style={labelStyle}>Adicionar Seções</label>
                    <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: "8px" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("hero")} style={blockBtnStyle}>➕ Hero</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("vitrine")} style={blockBtnStyle}>➕ Vitrine</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("textoImagem")} style={blockBtnStyle}>➕ Texto+Foto</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("depoimentos")} style={blockBtnStyle}>💬 Depoimentos</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("faq")} style={blockBtnStyle}>❓ FAQ</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("rodape")} style={blockBtnStyle}>👣 Rodapé</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={groupStyle}>
                    <label style={labelStyle}>Fundo do Topo</label>
                    <input
                        type="file"
                        accept="image/*"
                        onChange={handleHeroImageUpload}
                        style={{ fontSize: "11px", marginBottom: "5px" }}
                    />
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "10px", alignItems: "center" }}>
                        <input type="color" value={heroBg1} onChange={(e) => setHeroBg1(e.target.value)} style={colorCircle} />
                        <input type="color" value={heroBg2} onChange={(e) => setHeroBg2(e.target.value)} style={colorCircle} />
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "5px", marginTop: "5px" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => setHeroDirection("to right")} style={heroDirection === "to right" ? activeSmallBtn : smallBtnStyle}>👉</button>
                        <button onClick={() => setHeroDirection("to left")} style={heroDirection === "to left" ? activeSmallBtn : smallBtnStyle}>👈</button>
                        <button onClick={() => setHeroDirection("to bottom")} style={heroDirection === "to bottom" ? activeSmallBtn : smallBtnStyle}>👇</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={groupStyle}>
                    <label style={labelStyle}>Alinhamento</label>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "5px" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => changeHeroAlignment("left")} style={smallBtnStyle}>⬅️</button>
                        <button onClick={() => changeHeroAlignment("center")} style={smallBtnStyle}>⏺️</button>
                        <button onClick={() => changeHeroAlignment("right")} style={smallBtnStyle}>➡️</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={{ marginTop: "auto" }}>
                    <button onClick={() => navigate("/publicado")} style={btnStyle}>👁️ Ver Resultado</button>
                    <button
                        onClick={handleSave}
                        style={{ ...btnStyle, background: "#10b981", color: "white", border: "none" }}
                    >
                        💾 Salvar Agora
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div style={mainAreaStyle}>
                <div style={toolbarStyle}>
                    <button onClick={() => applyStyle("bold")} style={toolbarBtn}><b>B</b></button>
                    <button onClick={() => applyStyle("italic")} style={toolbarBtn}><i>I</i></button>
                    <input type="color" onChange={(e) => applyStyle("foreColor", e.target.value)} style={colorCircle} />
                </div>
                <iframe ref={iframeRef} style={iframeStyle} title="Editor" />
            </div>

            {isGalleryOpen && (
                <div style={modalOverlayStyle}>
                    <div style={modalContentStyle}>
                        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: "15px" }}>
                            <h3 style={{ margin: 0 }}>Galeria do Banco</h3>
                            <button
                                onClick={() => setIsGalleryOpen(false)}
                                style={{ border: "none", background: "none", cursor: "pointer", fontSize: "18px" }}
                            >
                                ✕
                            </button>
                        </div>
                        <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr 1fr", gap: "10px" }}>
                            {meusProdutosDoBanco.map((prod) => (
                                <img
                                    key={prod.id}
                                    src={prod.url}
                                    onClick={() => selectPhotoFromDatabase(prod.url)}
                                    style={galleryImgStyle}
                                    alt={`Produto ${prod.id}`}
                                />
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

O ponto principal que faltava era: os cards também precisavam do mesmo padrão do hero, ou seja, botão visual por fora e texto editável por dentro. Assim ele não quebra quando apaga e reescreve.

Se você quiser, no próximo passo eu posso te devolver uma versão com duplicar card, excluir card e adicionar novo produto dentro da vitrine.


ficou quase perfeito , ao selecionar o botão o color fica branco não dá pra ver  e o prço ou price , não esta editavel 

Perfeito. Faltam só 2 ajustes:

o texto do botão fica branco quando seleciona, então o foco precisa ter um estilo diferente para não “sumir”

o preço não está editável porque o clique no botão/estrutura pode estar capturando foco antes, e o <p class="product-price"> precisa ser explicitamente tratado como editável e clicável sem interferência

Abaixo está o que você deve trocar.

1) Corrigir o foco do botão branco
No seu CSS do fullHtml, troque este bloco:

[contenteditable]:focus {
    outline: 2px solid #b8917b;
    background: rgba(184, 145, 123, 0.05);
}
por este:

[contenteditable]:focus {
    outline: 2px solid #b8917b;
    outline-offset: 2px;
    background: rgba(184, 145, 123, 0.08);
}

.cta-button-text:focus,
.buy-button-text:focus {
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.22) !important;
    border-radius: 4px;
    padding: 2px 4px;
}

.product-price:focus {
    background: rgba(184, 145, 123, 0.12) !important;
    border-radius: 4px;
    padding: 2px 4px;
}
O que isso resolve
no botão, quando clicar no texto, aparece um fundo leve por trás

continua branco, mas agora fica visível

no preço, o foco também fica mais evidente

2) Fazer o preço realmente editável
No seu COMPONENT_LIBRARY.vitrine, troque o bloco do preço por este:

<p
    contenteditable="true"
    class="product-price"
    style="display:inline-block; color: #b8917b; font-weight: bold; font-size: 20px; margin-bottom: 15px;"
>
    R$ 00,00
</p>
O detalhe importante aqui é:

style="display:inline-block; ..."
Isso ajuda muito o clique e o foco no contenteditable.

3) Adicionar proteção no script para focar preço corretamente
No <script> do fullHtml, dentro do seu document.addEventListener("click", ...), adicione este bloco antes do bloco do .cta-button:

if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("product-price")) {
    target.focus();

    const range = document.createRange();
    range.selectNodeContents(target);
    range.collapse(false);

    const sel = window.getSelection();
    sel.removeAllRanges();
    sel.addRange(range);
    return;
}
E também adicione esta versão que cobre clique em filho/texto interno:

if (target.closest && target.closest(".product-price")) {
    const priceEl = target.closest(".product-price");
    priceEl.focus();

    const range = document.createRange();
    range.selectNodeContents(priceEl);
    range.collapse(false);

    const sel = window.getSelection();
    sel.removeAllRanges();
    sel.addRange(range);
    return;
}
4) Garantir que o preço entre na lista de editáveis
Na função enableEditableText(), deixe assim:

function enableEditableText() {
    const editableSelectors = [
        "h1", "h2", "h3", "h4", "h5", "h6",
        "p", "span", "small", "strong", "em",
        "li", "blockquote", "figcaption",
        ".cta-button-text",
        ".buy-button-text",
        ".product-price"
    ];

    document.querySelectorAll(editableSelectors.join(",")).forEach((el) => {
        if (
            !el.closest(".del-btn") &&
            !el.classList.contains("social-icon") &&
            !el.classList.contains("buy-button") &&
            !el.classList.contains("cta-button")
        ) {
            el.setAttribute("contenteditable", "true");
        }
    });
}
5) Trecho completo do click já corrigido
Substitua seu document.addEventListener("click", function (e) { ... }) por este:

document.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
    const target = e.target;

    if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("product-img")) {
        const el = target;
        const id = el.id || "img-" + Date.now();
        el.id = id;
        window.parent.postMessage({ type: "OPEN_GALLERY", elementId: id }, "*");
        return;
    }

    if (target.closest && target.closest(".product-img")) {
        const el = target.closest(".product-img");
        const id = el.id || "img-" + Date.now();
        el.id = id;
        window.parent.postMessage({ type: "OPEN_GALLERY", elementId: id }, "*");
        return;
    }

    if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("product-price")) {
        target.focus();

        const range = document.createRange();
        range.selectNodeContents(target);
        range.collapse(false);

        const sel = window.getSelection();
        sel.removeAllRanges();
        sel.addRange(range);
        return;
    }

    if (target.closest && target.closest(".product-price")) {
        const priceEl = target.closest(".product-price");
        priceEl.focus();

        const range = document.createRange();
        range.selectNodeContents(priceEl);
        range.collapse(false);

        const sel = window.getSelection();
        sel.removeAllRanges();
        sel.addRange(range);
        return;
    }

    if (target.tagName === "IMG") {
        const input = document.createElement("input");
        input.type = "file";
        input.accept = "image/*";
        input.onchange = function (ev) {
            const file = ev.target.files && ev.target.files[0];
            if (!file) return;

            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = function (re) {
                target.src = re.target.result;
            };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        };
        input.click();
        return;
    }

    if (target.closest && target.closest(".logo-placeholder")) {
        const placeholder = target.closest(".logo-placeholder");
        const input = document.createElement("input");
        input.type = "file";
        input.accept = "image/*";
        input.onchange = function (ev) {
            const file = ev.target.files && ev.target.files[0];
            if (!file) return;

            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = function (re) {
                placeholder.innerHTML = '<img src="' + re.target.result + '" style="max-width:100%; max-height:100%; width:auto; height:auto; object-fit:contain;" />';
            };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        };
        input.click();
        return;
    }

    if (target.closest && target.closest(".cta-button")) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const buttonText = target.classList && target.classList.contains("cta-button-text")
            ? target
            : target.closest(".cta-button").querySelector(".cta-button-text");

        if (buttonText) {
            buttonText.focus();

            const range = document.createRange();
            range.selectNodeContents(buttonText);
            range.collapse(false);

            const sel = window.getSelection();
            sel.removeAllRanges();
            sel.addRange(range);
        }
        return;
    }

    if (target.closest && target.closest(".buy-button")) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const buttonText = target.classList && target.classList.contains("buy-button-text")
            ? target
            : target.closest(".buy-button").querySelector(".buy-button-text");

        if (buttonText) {
            buttonText.focus();

            const range = document.createRange();
            range.selectNodeContents(buttonText);
            range.collapse(false);

            const sel = window.getSelection();
            sel.removeAllRanges();
            sel.addRange(range);
        }
        return;
    }
}, true);
6) Trecho final do preço dentro da vitrine
Use exatamente assim:

<p
    contenteditable="true"
    class="product-price"
    style="display:inline-block; color: #b8917b; font-weight: bold; font-size: 20px; margin-bottom: 15px;"
>
    R$ 00,00
</p>
Resumo do que faltava
O botão branco era só problema visual de foco.
O preço não editava porque faltava:

display:inline-block

foco manual no clique

estilo de foco mais claro

Se quiser, eu já posso te devolver o arquivo inteiro completo com esses 2 ajustes aplicados no código todo.

    const [heroBg2, setHeroBg2] = useState("#3a2b22");
    const [heroDirection, setHeroDirection] = useState("to right");

    const applyStyle = (command: string, value: string | null = null) => {
        iframeRef.current?.contentDocument?.execCommand(command, false, value || "");
    };

    const addComponent = (key: keyof typeof COMPONENT_LIBRARY) => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        if (doc) {
            const container = doc.querySelector(".luxury-container") || doc.body;
            const tempDiv = doc.createElement("div");
            tempDiv.innerHTML = COMPONENT_LIBRARY[key];

            const newEl = tempDiv.firstElementChild as HTMLElement | null;
            if (newEl) {
                container.appendChild(newEl);

                newEl
                    .querySelectorAll(
                        "h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, p, span, small, strong, em, li, blockquote, figcaption, .cta-button-text, .buy-button-text, .product-price"
                    )
                    .forEach((el) => {
                        const htmlEl = el as HTMLElement;
                        if (
                            !htmlEl.classList.contains("social-icon") &&
                            !htmlEl.classList.contains("buy-button") &&
                            !htmlEl.classList.contains("cta-button")
                        ) {
                            htmlEl.setAttribute("contenteditable", "true");
                        }
                    });
            }
        }
    };

    const updateHeroBackground = (style: string) => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        const hero = doc?.querySelector(".luxury-hero") as HTMLElement | null;
        if (hero) {
            hero.style.backgroundImage = style;
            hero.style.backgroundColor = "transparent";
            hero.style.backgroundSize = "cover";
            hero.style.backgroundPosition = "center";
            hero.style.backgroundRepeat = "no-repeat";
        }
    };

    const handleHeroImageUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (file) {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = (event) => {
                const res = event.target?.result;
                if (typeof res === "string") updateHeroBackground(url(${res}));
            };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        }
    };

    const changeHeroAlignment = (align: "left" | "center" | "right") => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        const hero = doc?.querySelector(".luxury-hero") as HTMLElement | null;
        if (hero) {
            hero.classList.remove("hero-align-left", "hero-align-center", "hero-align-right");
            hero.classList.add(hero-align-${align});

            const el = doc.getElementById(activeElementId) as HTMLElement | null;
            if (el) {
                if (el.tagName === "IMG") {
                    (el as HTMLImageElement).src = url;

                    el.style.backgroundImage = url(${url});
                    el.style.backgroundSize = "cover";
                    el.style.backgroundPosition = "center";
                }
                setIsGalleryOpen(false);
            }
        }
    };

                setActiveElementId(event.data.elementId);
                setIsGalleryOpen(true);
            }
        };
        window.addEventListener("message", handleMessage);
        return () => window.removeEventListener("message", handleMessage);
