        <script>
            function enableEditableText() {
                const editableSelectors = [
                    "h1", "h2", "h3", "h4", "h5", "h6",
                    "p", "span", "small", "strong", "em",
                    "li", "blockquote", "figcaption",
                    ".cta-button-text",
                    ".buy-button-text",
                    ".product-price"
                ];

                document.querySelectorAll(editableSelectors.join(",")).forEach((el) => {
                    if (
                        !el.closest(".del-btn") &&
                        !el.classList.contains("social-icon") &&
                        !el.classList.contains("buy-button") &&
                        !el.classList.contains("cta-button")
                    ) {
                        el.setAttribute("contenteditable", "true");
                    }
                });
            }

            enableEditableText();

            document.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
                const target = e.target;

                if (
                    target.classList &&
                    (
                        target.classList.contains("cta-button-text") ||
                        target.classList.contains("buy-button-text")
                    )
                ) {
                    if (e.key === "Enter") {
                        e.preventDefault();
                    }
                }
            });

            document.addEventListener("paste", function (e) {
                const target = e.target;

                if (
                    target.classList &&
                    (
                        target.classList.contains("cta-button-text") ||
                        target.classList.contains("buy-button-text")
                    )
                ) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const text = (e.clipboardData || window.clipboardData).getData("text");
                    document.execCommand("insertText", false, text);
                }
            });

            document.addEventListener("blur", function (e) {
                const target = e.target;

                if (
                    target.classList &&
                    target.classList.contains("cta-button-text") &&
                    target.textContent.trim() === ""
                ) {
                    target.textContent = "";
                }

                if (
                    target.classList &&
                    target.classList.contains("buy-button-text") &&
                    target.textContent.trim() === ""
                ) {
                    target.textContent = "";
                }
            }, true);

            document.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
                const target = e.target;

                if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("product-img")) {
                    const el = target;
                    const id = el.id || "img-" + Date.now();
                    el.id = id;
                    window.parent.postMessage({ type: "OPEN_GALLERY", elementId: id }, "*");
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".product-img")) {
                    const el = target.closest(".product-img");
                    const id = el.id || "img-" + Date.now();
                    el.id = id;
                    window.parent.postMessage({ type: "OPEN_GALLERY", elementId: id }, "*");
                    return;
                }

                if (target.tagName === "IMG") {
                    const input = document.createElement("input");
                    input.type = "file";
                    input.accept = "image/*";
                    input.onchange = function (ev) {
                        const file = ev.target.files && ev.target.files[0];
                        if (!file) return;

                        const reader = new FileReader();
                        reader.onload = function (re) {
                            target.src = re.target.result;
                        };
                        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                    };
                    input.click();
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".logo-placeholder")) {
                    const placeholder = target.closest(".logo-placeholder");
                    const input = document.createElement("input");
                    input.type = "file";
                    input.accept = "image/*";
                    input.onchange = function (ev) {
                        const file = ev.target.files && ev.target.files[0];
                        if (!file) return;

                        const reader = new FileReader();
                        reader.onload = function (re) {
                            placeholder.innerHTML = '<img src="' + re.target.result + '" style="max-width:100%; max-height:100%; width:auto; height:auto; object-fit:contain;" />';
                        };
                        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                    };
                    input.click();
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".cta-button")) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const buttonText = target.classList && target.classList.contains("cta-button-text")
                        ? target
                        : target.closest(".cta-button").querySelector(".cta-button-text");

                    if (buttonText) {
                        buttonText.focus();

                        const range = document.createRange();
                        range.selectNodeContents(buttonText);
                        range.collapse(false);

                        const sel = window.getSelection();
                        sel.removeAllRanges();
                        sel.addRange(range);
                    }
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".buy-button")) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const buttonText = target.classList && target.classList.contains("buy-button-text")
                        ? target
                        : target.closest(".buy-button").querySelector(".buy-button-text");

                    if (buttonText) {
                        buttonText.focus();

                        const range = document.createRange();
                        range.selectNodeContents(buttonText);
                        range.collapse(false);

                        const sel = window.getSelection();
                        sel.removeAllRanges();
                        sel.addRange(range);
                    }
                    return;
                }
            }, true);

            document.addEventListener("mouseover", function (e) {
                const section = e.target.closest && e.target.closest("section, footer");
                if (section && !section.querySelector(".del-btn")) {
                    const btn = document.createElement("button");
                    btn.innerHTML = "✕";
                    btn.className = "del-btn";
                    btn.contentEditable = "false";
                    btn.onclick = function (ev) {
                        ev.preventDefault();
                        ev.stopPropagation();
                        section.remove();
                    };
                    section.appendChild(btn);
                }
            });
        </script>
    </body>
</html>;

        iframe.srcdoc = fullHtml;
    }, [result.html, result.css]);

    return (
        <div style={containerStyle}>
            <div style={sidebarStyle}>
                <h3 style={{ fontSize: "16px", marginBottom: "10px" }}>Estúdio Design</h3>

                <div style={groupStyle}>
                    <label style={labelStyle}>Adicionar Seções</label>
                    <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: "8px" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("hero")} style={blockBtnStyle}>➕ Hero</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("vitrine")} style={blockBtnStyle}>➕ Vitrine</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("textoImagem")} style={blockBtnStyle}>➕ Texto+Foto</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("depoimentos")} style={blockBtnStyle}>💬 Depoimentos</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("faq")} style={blockBtnStyle}>❓ FAQ</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("rodape")} style={blockBtnStyle}>👣 Rodapé</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={groupStyle}>
                    <label style={labelStyle}>Fundo do Topo</label>
                    <input
                        type="file"
                        accept="image/*"
                        onChange={handleHeroImageUpload}
                        style={{ fontSize: "11px", marginBottom: "5px" }}
                    />
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "10px", alignItems: "center" }}>
                        <input type="color" value={heroBg1} onChange={(e) => setHeroBg1(e.target.value)} style={colorCircle} />
                        <input type="color" value={heroBg2} onChange={(e) => setHeroBg2(e.target.value)} style={colorCircle} />
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "5px", marginTop: "5px" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => setHeroDirection("to right")} style={heroDirection === "to right" ? activeSmallBtn : smallBtnStyle}>👉</button>
                        <button onClick={() => setHeroDirection("to left")} style={heroDirection === "to left" ? activeSmallBtn : smallBtnStyle}>👈</button>
                        <button onClick={() => setHeroDirection("to bottom")} style={heroDirection === "to bottom" ? activeSmallBtn : smallBtnStyle}>👇</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={groupStyle}>
                    <label style={labelStyle}>Alinhamento</label>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "5px" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => changeHeroAlignment("left")} style={smallBtnStyle}>⬅️</button>
                        <button onClick={() => changeHeroAlignment("center")} style={smallBtnStyle}>⏺️</button>
                        <button onClick={() => changeHeroAlignment("right")} style={smallBtnStyle}>➡️</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={{ marginTop: "auto" }}>
                    <button onClick={() => navigate("/publicado")} style={btnStyle}>👁️ Ver Resultado</button>
                    <button
                        onClick={handleSave}
                        style={{ ...btnStyle, background: "#10b981", color: "white", border: "none" }}
                    >
                        💾 Salvar Agora
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div style={mainAreaStyle}>
                <div style={toolbarStyle}>
                    <button onClick={() => applyStyle("bold")} style={toolbarBtn}><b>B</b></button>
                    <button onClick={() => applyStyle("italic")} style={toolbarBtn}><i>I</i></button>
                    <input type="color" onChange={(e) => applyStyle("foreColor", e.target.value)} style={colorCircle} />
                </div>
                <iframe ref={iframeRef} style={iframeStyle} title="Editor" />
            </div>

            {isGalleryOpen && (
                <div style={modalOverlayStyle}>
                    <div style={modalContentStyle}>
                        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: "15px" }}>
                            <h3 style={{ margin: 0 }}>Galeria do Banco</h3>
                            <button
                                onClick={() => setIsGalleryOpen(false)}
                                style={{ border: "none", background: "none", cursor: "pointer", fontSize: "18px" }}
                            >
                                ✕
                            </button>
                        </div>
                        <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr 1fr", gap: "10px" }}>
                            {meusProdutosDoBanco.map((prod) => (
                                <img
                                    key={prod.id}
                                    src={prod.url}
                                    onClick={() => selectPhotoFromDatabase(prod.url)}
                                    style={galleryImgStyle}
                                    alt={Produto ${prod.id}}
                                />
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

Sim — achei o erro principal.

O erro está aqui
No código que você colou, o bloco que faz foco no preço não foi adicionado dentro do click do iframe.

No seu document.addEventListener("click", ...), você tem tratamento para:

.product-img

IMG

.logo-placeholder

.cta-button

.buy-button

Mas não tem este tratamento:

if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("product-price")) {
   ...
}
Então o preço nunca recebe foco/cursor corretamente.

O que precisa corrigir
1. No HTML da vitrine
Seu preço está assim:

<p
    contenteditable="true"
    class="product-price"
    style="color: #b8917b; font-weight: bold; font-size: 20px; margin-bottom: 15px;"
>
    R$ 00,00
</p>
Troque para:

<p
    contenteditable="true"
    class="product-price"
    style="display:inline-block; color: #b8917b; font-weight: bold; font-size: 20px; margin-bottom: 15px;"
>
    R$ 00,00
</p>
O display:inline-block ajuda bastante o clique e seleção.

2. No CSS do iframe
Adicione também:

.product-price {
    cursor: text;
}

.cta-button-text:focus,
.buy-button-text:focus {
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.22) !important;
    border-radius: 4px;
    padding: 2px 4px;
}

.product-price:focus {
    background: rgba(184, 145, 123, 0.12) !important;
    border-radius: 4px;
    padding: 2px 4px;
}
No seu código isso já está quase lá, mas faltou o cursor: text no .product-price.

3. O principal: adicionar o bloco do preço no click
Dentro do seu script do iframe, no trecho:

document.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
    const target = e.target;
adicione logo depois dos blocos de .product-img e antes do if (target.tagName === "IMG") este trecho:

if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("product-price")) {
    target.focus();

    const range = document.createRange();
    range.selectNodeContents(target);
    range.collapse(false);

    const sel = window.getSelection();
    sel.removeAllRanges();
    sel.addRange(range);
    return;
}

if (target.closest && target.closest(".product-price")) {
    const priceEl = target.closest(".product-price");
    priceEl.focus();

    const range = document.createRange();
    range.selectNodeContents(priceEl);
    range.collapse(false);

    const sel = window.getSelection();
    sel.removeAllRanges();
    sel.addRange(range);
    return;
}
Onde exatamente está faltando no seu código
No seu click, hoje está assim:

if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("product-img")) { ... }

if (target.closest && target.closest(".product-img")) { ... }

if (target.tagName === "IMG") { ... }
Percebe? Ele pula direto para IMG.
O bloco do .product-price não existe aí.

Outro detalhe importante
No seu useEffect do iframe, as dependências estão assim:

}, [result.html, result.css]);
Mas você altera o hero com:

heroBg1

heroBg2

heroDirection

e esses estilos são aplicados depois com updateHeroBackground, então isso está ok.

O problema do preço não é React nem estado.
É só que o evento de clique dele não foi programado.

Patch direto no seu código
Troque o preço da vitrine por este
<p
    contenteditable="true"
    class="product-price"
    style="display:inline-block; color: #b8917b; font-weight: bold; font-size: 20px; margin-bottom: 15px;"
>
    R$ 00,00
</p>
Adicione no CSS
.product-price {
    cursor: text;
}

.cta-button-text:focus,
.buy-button-text:focus {
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.22) !important;
    border-radius: 4px;
    padding: 2px 4px;
}

.product-price:focus {
    background: rgba(184, 145, 123, 0.12) !important;
    border-radius: 4px;
    padding: 2px 4px;
}
E no document.addEventListener("click", ...)
cole este bloco:

if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("product-price")) {
    target.focus();

    const range = document.createRange();
    range.selectNodeContents(target);
    range.collapse(false);

    const sel = window.getSelection();
    sel.removeAllRanges();
    sel.addRange(range);
    return;
}

if (target.closest && target.closest(".product-price")) {
    const priceEl = target.closest(".product-price");
    priceEl.focus();

    const range = document.createRange();
    range.selectNodeContents(priceEl);
    range.collapse(false);

    const sel = window.getSelection();
    sel.removeAllRanges();
    sel.addRange(range);
    return;
}
Resumo exato do bug
O bug não era no contenteditable.
O bug foi que no código final:

você não colocou o handler de clique do .product-price

o preço ficou sem foco manual

o cursor nunca entrava nele corretamente

E no botão branco, faltava só um foco visual mais forte.

Se você quiser, eu posso te devolver o arquivo inteiro já com esse patch aplicado exatamente no seu código, sem mudar mais nada.

export default function Preview() {
    const { result: contextResult } = useResult();
    const location = useLocation();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const iframeRef = useRef<HTMLIFrameElement>(null);

    const result = location.state || contextResult || { html: "", css: "" };
    console.log("resultadoooooooooooooooo ", result);

    const [isGalleryOpen, setIsGalleryOpen] = useState(false);
    const [activeElementId, setActiveElementId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [heroBg1, setHeroBg1] = useState("#1a1a1a");
    const [heroBg2, setHeroBg2] = useState("#3a2b22");
    const [heroDirection, setHeroDirection] = useState("to right");

    const applyStyle = (command: string, value: string | null = null) => {
        iframeRef.current?.contentDocument?.execCommand(command, false, value || "");
    };

    const addComponent = (key: keyof typeof COMPONENT_LIBRARY) => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        if (doc) {
            const container = doc.querySelector(".luxury-container") || doc.body;
            const tempDiv = doc.createElement("div");
            tempDiv.innerHTML = COMPONENT_LIBRARY[key];

            const newEl = tempDiv.firstElementChild as HTMLElement | null;
            if (newEl) {
                container.appendChild(newEl);

                newEl
                    .querySelectorAll(
                        "h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, p, span, small, strong, em, li, blockquote, figcaption, .cta-button-text, .buy-button-text, .product-price"
                    )
                    .forEach((el) => {
                        const htmlEl = el as HTMLElement;
                        if (
                            !htmlEl.classList.contains("social-icon") &&
                            !htmlEl.classList.contains("buy-button") &&
                            !htmlEl.classList.contains("cta-button")
                        ) {
                            htmlEl.setAttribute("contenteditable", "true");
                        }
                    });
            }
        }
    };

    const updateHeroBackground = (style: string) => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        const hero = doc?.querySelector(".luxury-hero") as HTMLElement | null;
        if (hero) {
            hero.style.backgroundImage = style;
            hero.style.backgroundColor = "transparent";
            hero.style.backgroundSize = "cover";
            hero.style.backgroundPosition = "center";
            hero.style.backgroundRepeat = "no-repeat";
        }
    };

    const handleHeroImageUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (file) {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = (event) => {
                const res = event.target?.result;
                if (typeof res === "string") updateHeroBackground(`url(${res})`);
            };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        }
    };

    const changeHeroAlignment = (align: "left" | "center" | "right") => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        const hero = doc?.querySelector(".luxury-hero") as HTMLElement | null;
        if (hero) {
            hero.classList.remove("hero-align-left", "hero-align-center", "hero-align-right");
            hero.classList.add(`hero-align-${align}`);
        }
    };

    const selectPhotoFromDatabase = (url: string) => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        if (doc && activeElementId) {
            const el = doc.getElementById(activeElementId) as HTMLElement | null;
            if (el) {
                if (el.tagName === "IMG") {
                    (el as HTMLImageElement).src = url;
                } else {
                    el.style.backgroundImage = `url(${url})`;
                    el.style.backgroundSize = "cover";
                    el.style.backgroundPosition = "center";
                }
                setIsGalleryOpen(false);
            }
        }
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        updateHeroBackground(`linear-gradient(${heroDirection}, ${heroBg1}, ${heroBg2})`);
    }, [heroBg1, heroBg2, heroDirection]);

    useEffect(() => {
        const handleMessage = (event: MessageEvent) => {
            if (event.data.type === "OPEN_GALLERY") {
                setActiveElementId(event.data.elementId);
                setIsGalleryOpen(true);
            }
        };
        window.addEventListener("message", handleMessage);
        return () => window.removeEventListener("message", handleMessage);
    }, []);

    const handleSave = () => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        if (doc) {
            const cleanHtml = doc.body.cloneNode(true) as HTMLElement;

            cleanHtml.querySelectorAll(".del-btn").forEach((btn) => btn.remove());

            cleanHtml.querySelectorAll("[contenteditable]").forEach((el) => {
                el.removeAttribute("contenteditable");
                (el as HTMLElement).style.outline = "none";
            });

            const content =
                cleanHtml.querySelector(".luxury-container")?.innerHTML || cleanHtml.innerHTML;

            const dataToSave = {
                html: content,
                css: result.css,
                usuarioId: "user_2026_99",
                condominioId: null,
            };

            localStorage.setItem("landing_page_draft", JSON.stringify(dataToSave));
            alert("Landing Page salva com sucesso!");
        }
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        const iframe = iframeRef.current;
        if (!iframe) return;

        let bodyContent = result.html || "";
        const bodyMatch = bodyContent.match(/<body[^>]*>([\\s\\S]*)<\\/body>/i);
        if (bodyMatch && bodyMatch[1]) {
            bodyContent = bodyMatch[1];
        }

        const fullHtml = `
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
    <head>
        <meta charset="UTF-8">
        <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Playfair+Display:wght@600&family=Great+Vibes&family=Inter:wght@400;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
        <style>
            ${result.css || ""}
            body {
                margin: 0;
                padding: 0;
                min-height: 100vh;
                font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            }

            .luxury-container {
                min-height: 100vh;
                width: 100%;
                position: relative;
            }

            img {
                max-width: 100%;
                height: auto;
            }

            [contenteditable]:focus {
                outline: 2px solid #b8917b;
                outline-offset: 2px;
                background: rgba(184, 145, 123, 0.08);
            }

            .del-btn {
                opacity: 0;
                transition: 0.2s;
                position: absolute;
                top: 10px;
                right: 10px;
                background: red;
                color: white;
                border: none;
                width: 25px;
                height: 25px;
                cursor: pointer;
                border-radius: 50%;
                z-index: 100;
            }

            section:hover .del-btn,
            footer:hover .del-btn {
                opacity: 1;
            }

            section,
            footer {
                position: relative;
            }

            .hero-align-left > div {
                text-align: left;
            }

            .hero-align-center > div {
                text-align: center;
            }

            .hero-align-right > div {
                text-align: right;
            }

            .cta-button {
                display: inline-flex !important;
                align-items: center;
                justify-content: center;
                min-width: 140px;
                min-height: 50px;
                padding: 15px 40px;
                background: #b8917b;
                color: #fff !important;
                border-radius: 4px;
                font-weight: bold;
                font-size: 16px;
                text-decoration: none !important;
                border: none;
                cursor: pointer;
                vertical-align: middle;
            }

            .cta-button-text {
                display: inline-block;
                min-width: 24px;
                min-height: 20px;
                color: inherit !important;
                font: inherit;
                font-weight: inherit;
                line-height: inherit;
                white-space: nowrap;
                outline: none;
            }

            .cta-button-text:empty::before {
                content: "CLIQUE AQUI";
                opacity: 0.55;
                color: inherit;
                font: inherit;
                font-weight: inherit;
            }

            .buy-button {
                display: flex;
                justify-content: center;
                align-items: center;
                width: 100%;
                padding: 12px;
                background: #b8917b;
                color: white;
                border: none;
                cursor: pointer;
                border-radius: 6px;
                font-weight: bold;
            }

            .buy-button-text {
                display: inline-block;
                min-width: 10px;
                min-height: 18px;
                outline: none;
                color: inherit !important;
                font: inherit;
                font-weight: inherit;
                line-height: inherit;
                white-space: nowrap;
            }

            .buy-button-text:empty::before {
                content: "Comprar";
                opacity: 0.5;
                color: inherit;
                font: inherit;
                font-weight: inherit;
            }

            .product-price {
                cursor: text;
            }

            .cta-button-text:focus,
            .buy-button-text:focus {
                background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.22) !important;
                border-radius: 4px;
                padding: 2px 4px;
            }

            .product-price:focus {
                background: rgba(184, 145, 123, 0.12) !important;
                border-radius: 4px;
                padding: 2px 4px;
            }

            .product-img:hover,
            img:hover,
            .logo-placeholder:hover,
            .cta-button:hover,
            .buy-button:hover {
                outline: 2px dashed rgba(184, 145, 123, 0.65);
                outline-offset: 2px;
            }
        </style>
    </head>
    <body>
        <main class="luxury-container">
            ${bodyContent}
        </main>
        <script>
            function enableEditableText() {
                const editableSelectors = [
                    "h1", "h2", "h3", "h4", "h5", "h6",
                    "p", "span", "small", "strong", "em",
                    "li", "blockquote", "figcaption",
                    ".cta-button-text",
                    ".buy-button-text",
                    ".product-price"
                ];

                document.querySelectorAll(editableSelectors.join(",")).forEach((el) => {
                    if (
                        !el.closest(".del-btn") &&
                        !el.classList.contains("social-icon") &&
                        !el.classList.contains("buy-button") &&
                        !el.classList.contains("cta-button")
                    ) {
                        el.setAttribute("contenteditable", "true");
                    }
                });
            }

            enableEditableText();

            document.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
                const target = e.target;

                if (
                    target.classList &&
                    (
                        target.classList.contains("cta-button-text") ||
                        target.classList.contains("buy-button-text")
                    )
                ) {
                    if (e.key === "Enter") {
                        e.preventDefault();
                    }
                }

                const target = e.target;

                    target.classList &&
                    (
                        target.classList.contains("cta-button-text") ||
                        target.classList.contains("buy-button-text")

                    e.preventDefault();

                    document.execCommand("insertText", false, text);

            document.addEventListener("blur", function (e) {

                    target.classList &&
                    target.classList.contains("cta-button-text") &&
                    target.textContent.trim() === ""
                ) {
                    target.textContent = "";
                }

                    target.classList.contains("buy-button-text") &&
                    target.textContent.trim() === ""
                ) {
                    target.textContent = "";

            document.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
                const target = e.target;

                if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("product-img")) {
                    const el = target;
                    const id = el.id || "img-" + Date.now();
                    el.id = id;
                    window.parent.postMessage({ type: "OPEN_GALLERY", elementId: id }, "*");
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".product-img")) {
                    const el = target.closest(".product-img");
                    const id = el.id || "img-" + Date.now();
                    el.id = id;
                    window.parent.postMessage({ type: "OPEN_GALLERY", elementId: id }, "*");
                    return;
                }

                if (target.classList && target.classList.contains("product-price")) {
                    target.focus();

                    const range = document.createRange();
                    range.selectNodeContents(target);
                    range.collapse(false);

                    const sel = window.getSelection();
                    sel.removeAllRanges();
                    sel.addRange(range);
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".product-price")) {
                    const priceEl = target.closest(".product-price");
                    priceEl.focus();

                    const range = document.createRange();
                    range.selectNodeContents(priceEl);
                    range.collapse(false);

                    const sel = window.getSelection();
                    sel.removeAllRanges();
                    sel.addRange(range);
                    return;
                }

                if (target.tagName === "IMG") {
                    const input = document.createElement("input");
                    input.type = "file";
                    input.accept = "image/*";
                    input.onchange = function (ev) {
                        const file = ev.target.files && ev.target.files[0];
                        if (!file) return;

                        const reader = new FileReader();
                        reader.onload = function (re) {
                            target.src = re.target.result;
                        };
                        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                    };
                    input.click();
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".logo-placeholder")) {
                    const placeholder = target.closest(".logo-placeholder");
                    const input = document.createElement("input");
                    input.type = "file";
                    input.accept = "image/*";
                    input.onchange = function (ev) {
                        const file = ev.target.files && ev.target.files[0];
                        if (!file) return;

                        const reader = new FileReader();
                        reader.onload = function (re) {
                            placeholder.innerHTML = '<img src="' + re.target.result + '" style="max-width:100%; max-height:100%; width:auto; height:auto; object-fit:contain;" />';
                        };
                        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                    };
                    input.click();
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".cta-button")) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const buttonText = target.classList && target.classList.contains("cta-button-text")
                        ? target
                        : target.closest(".cta-button").querySelector(".cta-button-text");

                    if (buttonText) {
                        buttonText.focus();

                        const range = document.createRange();
                        range.selectNodeContents(buttonText);
                        range.collapse(false);

                        const sel = window.getSelection();
                        sel.removeAllRanges();
                        sel.addRange(range);
                    }
                    return;
                }

                if (target.closest && target.closest(".buy-button")) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const buttonText = target.classList && target.classList.contains("buy-button-text")
                        ? target
                        : target.closest(".buy-button").querySelector(".buy-button-text");

                    if (buttonText) {
                        buttonText.focus();

                        const range = document.createRange();
                        range.selectNodeContents(buttonText);
                        range.collapse(false);

                        const sel = window.getSelection();
                        sel.removeAllRanges();
                        sel.addRange(range);
                    }
                    return;
                }
            }, true);

            document.addEventListener("mouseover", function (e) {
                const section = e.target.closest && e.target.closest("section, footer");
                if (section && !section.querySelector(".del-btn")) {
                    const btn = document.createElement("button");
                    btn.innerHTML = "✕";
                    btn.className = "del-btn";
                    btn.contentEditable = "false";
                    btn.onclick = function (ev) {
                        ev.preventDefault();
                        ev.stopPropagation();
                        section.remove();
                    };
                    section.appendChild(btn);
                }
            });
        </script>
    </body>
</html>`;

        iframe.srcdoc = fullHtml;
    }, [result.html, result.css]);

    return (
        <div style={containerStyle}>
            <div style={sidebarStyle}>
                <h3 style={{ fontSize: "16px", marginBottom: "10px" }}>Estúdio Design</h3>

                <div style={groupStyle}>
                    <label style={labelStyle}>Adicionar Seções</label>
                    <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: "8px" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("hero")} style={blockBtnStyle}>➕ Hero</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("vitrine")} style={blockBtnStyle}>➕ Vitrine</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("textoImagem")} style={blockBtnStyle}>➕ Texto+Foto</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("depoimentos")} style={blockBtnStyle}>💬 Depoimentos</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("faq")} style={blockBtnStyle}>❓ FAQ</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent("rodape")} style={blockBtnStyle}>👣 Rodapé</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={groupStyle}>
                    <label style={labelStyle}>Fundo do Topo</label>
                    <input
                        type="file"
                        accept="image/*"
                        onChange={handleHeroImageUpload}
                        style={{ fontSize: "11px", marginBottom: "5px" }}
                    />
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "10px", alignItems: "center" }}>
                        <input type="color" value={heroBg1} onChange={(e) => setHeroBg1(e.target.value)} style={colorCircle} />
                        <input type="color" value={heroBg2} onChange={(e) => setHeroBg2(e.target.value)} style={colorCircle} />
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "5px", marginTop: "5px" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => setHeroDirection("to right")} style={heroDirection === "to right" ? activeSmallBtn : smallBtnStyle}>👉</button>
                        <button onClick={() => setHeroDirection("to left")} style={heroDirection === "to left" ? activeSmallBtn : smallBtnStyle}>👈</button>
                        <button onClick={() => setHeroDirection("to bottom")} style={heroDirection === "to bottom" ? activeSmallBtn : smallBtnStyle}>👇</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={groupStyle}>
                    <label style={labelStyle}>Alinhamento</label>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "5px" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => changeHeroAlignment("left")} style={smallBtnStyle}>⬅️</button>
                        <button onClick={() => changeHeroAlignment("center")} style={smallBtnStyle}>⏺️</button>
                        <button onClick={() => changeHeroAlignment("right")} style={smallBtnStyle}>➡️</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={{ marginTop: "auto" }}>
                    <button onClick={() => navigate("/publicado")} style={btnStyle}>👁️ Ver Resultado</button>
                    <button
                        onClick={handleSave}
                        style={{ ...btnStyle, background: "#10b981", color: "white", border: "none" }}
                    >
                        💾 Salvar Agora
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div style={mainAreaStyle}>
                <div style={toolbarStyle}>
                    <button onClick={() => applyStyle("bold")} style={toolbarBtn}><b>B</b></button>
                    <button onClick={() => applyStyle("italic")} style={toolbarBtn}><i>I</i></button>
                    <input type="color" onChange={(e) => applyStyle("foreColor", e.target.value)} style={colorCircle} />
                </div>
                <iframe ref={iframeRef} style={iframeStyle} title="Editor" />
            </div>

            {isGalleryOpen && (
                <div style={modalOverlayStyle}>
                    <div style={modalContentStyle}>
                        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: "15px" }}>
                            <h3 style={{ margin: 0 }}>Galeria do Banco</h3>
                            <button
                                onClick={() => setIsGalleryOpen(false)}
                                style={{ border: "none", background: "none", cursor: "pointer", fontSize: "18px" }}
                            >
                                ✕
                            </button>
                        </div>
                        <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr 1fr", gap: "10px" }}>
                            {meusProdutosDoBanco.map((prod) => (
                                <img
                                    key={prod.id}
                                    src={prod.url}
                                    onClick={() => selectPhotoFromDatabase(prod.url)}
                                    style={galleryImgStyle}
                                    alt={\`Produto \${prod.id}\`}
                                />
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

O ponto que faltava mesmo era o bloco de clique do .product-price dentro do script do iframe.

Se ainda assim o preço continuar sem entrar em edição, o próximo ponto a testar é se o HTML vindo em result.html está sobrescrevendo a vitrine que você montou no COMPONENT_LIBRARY.


tem algum erro aqui {meusProdutosDoBanco.map((prod) => (
                                <img
                                    key={prod.id}
                                    src={prod.url}
                                    onClick={() => selectPhotoFromDatabase(prod.url)}
                                    style={galleryImgStyle}
                                    alt={\Produto \${prod.id}\}
                                /> Unexpected token. Did you mean {'>'} or &gt;?ts(1382)
 
                            ))}

Sim. O erro está aqui:

alt={\`Produto \${prod.id}\`}
Você colocou barras invertidas \ antes da template string. Em TSX/JSX isso quebra o parser.

Correto
Use assim:

{meusProdutosDoBanco.map((prod) => (
    <img
        key={prod.id}
        src={prod.url}
        onClick={() => selectPhotoFromDatabase(prod.url)}
        style={galleryImgStyle}
        alt={`Produto ${prod.id}`}
    />
))}
Por que deu erro
Você escreveu:

alt={\`Produto \${prod.id}\`}
mas o correto é:

alt={`Produto ${prod.id}`}
Sem as barras invertidas.

Regra prática
JSX aceita {...} para JavaScript

template string usa crase normal:

`texto ${variavel}`
não precisa escapar a crase dentro do TSX nesse caso

Se quiser, eu posso revisar também o restante do arquivo para achar outros pontos que possam dar erro de sintaxe.


pode arrumar :        const bodyMatch = bodyContent.match(/<body[^>]*>([\\s\\S]*)<\\/body>);

Sim — esse regex está com escape duplicado (\\) e isso quebra quando usado dentro de código JavaScript normal.

O correto é usar apenas uma barra \.

Corrija assim
const bodyMatch = bodyContent.match(/<body[^>]*>([\s\S]*)<\/body>/i);
Explicação rápida
Você tinha:

/<body[^>]*>([\\s\\S]*)<\\/body>/
Isso acontece quando o regex foi copiado de dentro de uma string (ex: template string ou JSON), onde \ precisa virar \\.

Mas no JavaScript direto com regex literal /.../:

errado	correto
\\s	\s
\\S	\S
<\\/body>	<\/body>
Versão final correta
let bodyContent = result.html || "";

const bodyMatch = bodyContent.match(/<body[^>]*>([\s\S]*)<\/body>/i);

if (bodyMatch && bodyMatch[1]) {
    bodyContent = bodyMatch[1];
}
Dica
Esse regex pega tudo dentro do <body>, mesmo com quebras de linha.

[\s\S] = qualquer caractere (inclusive newline)
Se quiser, posso também te mostrar uma versão mais segura para extrair o body usando DOMParser, que evita erros quando o HTML vem quebrado da IA. Isso deixa seu editor muito mais robusto.


agora ao dar o focus no botão o text fica branco poderia deixar com uma cor que de pra ver 

Sim — isso acontece porque o botão tem:

color: white
Então quando você dá focus, o texto continua branco e o fundo também fica claro, ficando difícil de enxergar.

A solução é mudar a cor do texto apenas quando estiver em foco.

Correção simples (adicione no CSS do iframe)
Coloque depois do seu CSS existente:

.cta-button-text:focus,
.buy-button-text:focus {
    background: rgba(255,255,255,0.85);
    color: #1a1a1a !important;
    border-radius: 4px;
    padding: 2px 6px;
}
Resultado
Quando clicar no botão para editar:

Antes

CLIQUE AQUI
Durante edição

[CLIQUE AQUI]
fundo claro

texto escuro

cursor visível

Se quiser deixar ainda melhor (estilo editor profissional)
Use este:

.cta-button-text:focus,
.buy-button-text:focus {
    background: #fff;
    color: #111 !important;
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 3px 8px;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(184,145,123,0.35);
}
Fica parecido com editores como:

Webflow

Framer

Shopify editor

Dica importante para seu editor
Para evitar problemas futuros, você também pode garantir que o texto sempre herde o estilo do botão:

.cta-button-text,
.buy-button-text{
    color: inherit;
    font: inherit;
}
Se quiser, posso também te passar um pequeno upgrade de 10 linhas que permite:

✔ mudar cor do botão clicando nele
✔ mudar cor do texto do botão
✔ mudar tamanho do botão

igual aos editores de landing page profissionais.

export default function Preview() {
    const { result: contextResult } = useResult();
    const location = useLocation();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const iframeRef = useRef<HTMLIFrameElement>(null);

    const result = location.state || contextResult;
    console.log('resultadoooooooooooooooo ', result)

    const [isGalleryOpen, setIsGalleryOpen] = useState(false);
    const [activeElementId, setActiveElementId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [heroBg1, setHeroBg1] = useState("#1a1a1a");
    const [heroBg2, setHeroBg2] = useState("#3a2b22");
    const [heroDirection, setHeroDirection] = useState("to right");

    const applyStyle = (command: string, value: string | null = null) => {
        iframeRef.current?.contentDocument?.execCommand(command, false, value || "");
    };

    const addComponent = (key: keyof typeof COMPONENT_LIBRARY) => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        if (doc) {
            const container = doc.querySelector('.luxury-container') || doc.body;
            const tempDiv = doc.createElement('div');
            tempDiv.innerHTML = COMPONENT_LIBRARY[key];
            container.appendChild(tempDiv.firstElementChild!);
        }
    };

    const updateHeroBackground = (style: string) => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        const hero = doc?.querySelector(".luxury-hero") as HTMLElement;
        if (hero) {
            hero.style.backgroundImage = style;
            hero.style.backgroundColor = "transparent";
            hero.style.backgroundSize = "cover";
            hero.style.backgroundPosition = "center";
            hero.style.backgroundRepeat = "no-repeat";
        }
    };

    const handleHeroImageUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (file) {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = (event) => {
                const res = event.target?.result;
                if (typeof res === "string") updateHeroBackground(url(${res}));
            };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        }
    };

    const changeHeroAlignment = (align: "left" | "center" | "right") => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        const hero = doc?.querySelector(".luxury-hero") as HTMLElement;
        if (hero) {
            hero.classList.remove("hero-align-left", "hero-align-center", "hero-align-right");
            hero.classList.add(hero-align-${align});
        }
    };

    const selectPhotoFromDatabase = (url: string) => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        if (doc && activeElementId) {
            const el = doc.getElementById(activeElementId) as HTMLElement;
            if (el) {
                el.style.backgroundImage = url(${url});
                el.style.backgroundSize = "cover"; // Padroniza o preenchimento
                el.style.backgroundPosition = "center";
                setIsGalleryOpen(false);
            }
        }
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        updateHeroBackground(linear-gradient(${heroDirection}, ${heroBg1}, ${heroBg2}));
    }, [heroBg1, heroBg2, heroDirection]);

    useEffect(() => {
        const handleMessage = (event: MessageEvent) => {
            if (event.data.type === 'OPEN_GALLERY') {
                setActiveElementId(event.data.elementId);
                setIsGalleryOpen(true);
            }
        };
        window.addEventListener('message', handleMessage);
        return () => window.removeEventListener('message', handleMessage);
    }, []);

    const handleSave = () => {
        const doc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument;
        if (doc) {
            // Criamos uma cópia para não sujar o editor enquanto o usuário mexe
            const cleanHtml = doc.body.cloneNode(true) as HTMLElement;

            // 1. Limpeza de interface do editor
            cleanHtml.querySelectorAll('.del-btn').forEach(btn => btn.remove());
            cleanHtml.querySelectorAll('[contenteditable]').forEach(el => {
                el.removeAttribute('contenteditable');
                (el as HTMLElement).style.outline = 'none';
            });

            // 2. Captura Inteligente: 
            // Se existir .luxury-container (componentes adicionados manualmente), pega ele.
            // Se não (HTML vindo direto da IA), pega o body todo.
            const content = cleanHtml.querySelector('.luxury-container')?.innerHTML || cleanHtml.innerHTML;

            const dataToSave = {
                html: content,
                css: result.css,
                usuarioId: "user_2026_99", // Conforme seu padrão [2026-01-10]
                condominioId: null       // Opcional conforme instrução [2026-01-27]
            };

            localStorage.setItem("landing_page_draft", JSON.stringify(dataToSave));
            alert("Landing Page salva com sucesso!");
        }
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        const iframe = iframeRef.current;
        // IMPORTANTE: Só rodar se houver conteúdo e se o iframe existir
        if (!iframe || !result.html) return;

        const doc = iframe.contentDocument || iframe.contentWindow?.document;

        console.log('docccccccccc ', doc)
        if (!doc) return;

        // 1. Tratamento do conteúdo
        let bodyContent = result.html;
        const bodyMatch = bodyContent.match(/<body[^>]*>([\s\S]*)<\/body>/i);
        if (bodyMatch && bodyMatch[1]) {
            bodyContent = bodyMatch[1];
        }

        // 2. Construção do documento (Movido para fora para clareza)
        const fullHtml = 
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
    <head>
        <meta charset="UTF-8">
        <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Playfair+Display:wght@600&family=Great+Vibes&family=Inter:wght@400;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
        <style>
            ${result.css} 
            body { margin: 0; padding: 0; min-height: 100vh; font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif; }
            .luxury-container { min-height: 100vh; width: 100%; position: relative; }
            img { max-width: 100%; height: auto; }
            [contenteditable]:focus { outline: 2px solid #b8917b; background: rgba(184, 145, 123, 0.05); }
            .del-btn { opacity: 0; transition: 0.2s; position: absolute; top: 10px; right: 10px; background: red; color: white; border: none; width: 25px; height: 25px; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 50%; z-index: 100; }
            section:hover .del-btn, footer:hover .del-btn { opacity: 1; }
            section, footer { position: relative; }
        </style>
    </head>
    <body>
        <main class="luxury-container">
            ${bodyContent}
        </main>
        <script>
            // Injetamos o script diretamente aqui para evitar problemas de escopo
            document.body.contentEditable = 'true';
            
            document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                const target = e.target;
                if (target.classList.contains('product-img') || target.closest('.product-img')) {
                    const el = target.closest('.product-img');
                    const id = el.id || 'img-' + Date.now();
                    el.id = id;
                    window.parent.postMessage({ type: 'OPEN_GALLERY', elementId: id }, '*');
                } else if (target.tagName === 'IMG' || target.closest('.logo-placeholder')) {
                    const placeholder = target.closest('.logo-placeholder') || target;
                    const input = document.createElement('input');
                    input.type = 'file';
                    input.onchange = (ev) => {
                        const reader = new FileReader();
                        reader.onload = (re) => {
                            if(target.tagName === 'IMG') target.src = re.target.result;
                            else placeholder.innerHTML = '<img src="'+re.target.result+'" style="max-height:100%; object-fit:contain;">';
                        };
                        reader.readAsDataURL(ev.target.files[0]);
                    };
                    input.click();
                }
            }, true);

            // Gerenciador de deleção simplificado
            document.addEventListener('mouseover', (e) => {
                const section = e.target.closest('section, footer');
                if (section && !section.querySelector('.del-btn')) {
                    const btn = document.createElement('button');
                    btn.innerHTML = '✕'; btn.className = 'del-btn';
                    btn.onclick = () => section.remove();
                    section.appendChild(btn);
                }
            });
        </script>
    </body>
</html>;

        // 3. O Pulo do Gato: Forçar o carregamento
        // Usar srcdoc é mais estável no React do que doc.write em alguns navegadores
        iframe.srcdoc = fullHtml;

    }, [result.html, result.css]); // Removido o timeout e usado srcdoc
    // Dependências explícitas

    return (
        <div style={containerStyle}>
            <div style={sidebarStyle}>
                <h3 style={{ fontSize: "16px", marginBottom: "10px" }}>Estúdio Design</h3>

                <div style={groupStyle}>
                    <label style={labelStyle}>Adicionar Seções</label>
                    <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '8px' }}>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent('hero')} style={blockBtnStyle}>➕ Hero</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent('vitrine')} style={blockBtnStyle}>➕ Vitrine</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent('textoImagem')} style={blockBtnStyle}>➕ Texto+Foto</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent('depoimentos')} style={blockBtnStyle}>💬 Depoimentos</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent('faq')} style={blockBtnStyle}>❓ FAQ</button>
                        <button onClick={() => addComponent('rodape')} style={blockBtnStyle}>👣 Rodapé</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={groupStyle}>
                    <label style={labelStyle}>Fundo do Topo</label>
                    <input type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={handleHeroImageUpload} style={{ fontSize: "11px", marginBottom: '5px' }} />
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "10px", alignItems: "center" }}>
                        <input type="color" value={heroBg1} onChange={(e) => setHeroBg1(e.target.value)} style={colorCircle} />
                        <input type="color" value={heroBg2} onChange={(e) => setHeroBg2(e.target.value)} style={colorCircle} />
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "5px", marginTop: "5px" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => setHeroDirection("to right")} style={heroDirection === "to right" ? activeSmallBtn : smallBtnStyle}>👉</button>
                        <button onClick={() => setHeroDirection("to left")} style={heroDirection === "to left" ? activeSmallBtn : smallBtnStyle}>👈</button>
                        <button onClick={() => setHeroDirection("to bottom")} style={heroDirection === "to bottom" ? activeSmallBtn : smallBtnStyle}>👇</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={groupStyle}>
                    <label style={labelStyle}>Alinhamento</label>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "5px" }}>
                        <button onClick={() => changeHeroAlignment("left")} style={smallBtnStyle}>⬅️</button>
                        <button onClick={() => changeHeroAlignment("center")} style={smallBtnStyle}>⏺️</button>
                        <button onClick={() => changeHeroAlignment("right")} style={smallBtnStyle}>➡️</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={{ marginTop: "auto" }}>
                    <button onClick={() => navigate("/publicado")} style={btnStyle}>👁️ Ver Resultado</button>
                    <button onClick={handleSave} style={{ ...btnStyle, background: "#10b981", color: "white", border: "none" }}>💾 Salvar Agora</button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div style={mainAreaStyle}>
                <div style={toolbarStyle}>
                    <button onClick={() => applyStyle("bold")} style={toolbarBtn}><b>B</b></button>
                    <button onClick={() => applyStyle("italic")} style={toolbarBtn}><i>I</i></button>
                    <input type="color" onChange={(e) => applyStyle("foreColor", e.target.value)} style={colorCircle} />
                </div>
                <iframe ref={iframeRef} style={iframeStyle} title="Editor" />
            </div>

            {isGalleryOpen && (
                <div style={modalOverlayStyle}>
                    <div style={modalContentStyle}>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: '15px' }}>
                            <h3 style={{ margin: 0 }}>Galeria do Banco</h3>
                            <button onClick={() => setIsGalleryOpen(false)} style={{ border: 'none', background: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: '18px' }}>✕</button>
                        </div>
                        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr 1fr', gap: '10px' }}>
                            {meusProdutosDoBanco.map(prod => (
                                <img key={prod.id} src={prod.url} onClick={() => selectPhotoFromDatabase(prod.url)} style={galleryImgStyle} />
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

// Estilos
const containerStyle: React.CSSProperties = { display: "flex", background: "#f1f5f9", height: "100vh", fontFamily: 'sans-serif' };
const sidebarStyle: React.CSSProperties = { width: "280px", background: "white", padding: "20px", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: "15px", borderRight: "1px solid #ddd" };
const mainAreaStyle: React.CSSProperties = { flex: 1, padding: "20px", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column" };
const groupStyle: React.CSSProperties = { display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: "8px", padding: "12px", background: "#f8fafc", borderRadius: "8px" };
const labelStyle: React.CSSProperties = { fontSize: "11px", fontWeight: "bold", color: "#64748b", textTransform: "uppercase" };
const colorCircle: React.CSSProperties = { width: "26px", height: "26px", border: "1px solid #ddd", borderRadius: "50%", cursor: "pointer", padding: "0" };
const btnStyle: React.CSSProperties = { width: "100%", padding: "12px", borderRadius: "8px", border: "1px solid #ddd", cursor: "pointer", fontWeight: "bold", marginBottom: "8px", background: "#f1f5f9" };
const blockBtnStyle: React.CSSProperties = { padding: "8px", background: "white", border: "1px solid #cbd5e1", borderRadius: "6px", cursor: "pointer", fontSize: "11px", textAlign: 'left' };
const smallBtnStyle: React.CSSProperties = { flex: 1, padding: "8px", border: "1px solid #ddd", background: "white", cursor: "pointer", borderRadius: "4px" };
const activeSmallBtn: React.CSSProperties = { ...smallBtnStyle, background: "#3b82f6", color: "white", borderColor: "#3b82f6" };
const toolbarStyle: React.CSSProperties = { background: "white", padding: "10px", borderRadius: "8px", display: "flex", gap: "10px", marginBottom: "15px", boxShadow: "0 2px 5px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)", alignItems: "center" };
const toolbarBtn: React.CSSProperties = { padding: "5px 12px", border: "1px solid #ddd", background: "white", cursor: "pointer", borderRadius: "4px" };
const iframeStyle: React.CSSProperties = { width: "100%", height: "100%", border: "none", background: "white", borderRadius: "10px", boxShadow: "0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)" };

const modalContentStyle: React.CSSProperties = { background: 'white', padding: '20px', borderRadius: '12px', width: '450px', maxHeight: '80vh', overflowY: 'auto' };
